# Mystical Garden architecture

## Expedition suspension and guest rejoin

A solo player or co-op host can use Save & Quit during an expedition. A normal window close takes the same path. One versioned suspension belongs to the selected Garden and host character. A successful Continue Expedition reconstructs the floor and consumes the suspension before play resumes. Both operations use the existing Garden transaction and recovery rules. They introduce no periodic combat writes. An unexpected loss of the host process can still lose the active fight.

`expedition_continuation.gd` captures participation, player builds and cargo, enemies, queued combat, random streams, loot claims and expedition statistics from their existing owners. `expedition_suspend_codec.gd` reconstructs recognized value classes and preserves shared action identities. Combat time and participation timers exclude time spent suspended.

The host retains each unexpectedly disconnected participant by character identity. That actor is absent from simulation. Rejoin requires the same character's private credential for the host address and port. A new connection receives the retained build and cargo. Rejoining on the same floor preserves position. A later floor uses its arrival position and clears prior-floor effects. Explicit abandonment still settles immediately. At the end of the run, absent characters settle under the existing disconnection and protected-cargo rules.

## Working baseline

The integrated baseline is `a4eed49680a99f8441312fc3870b54acfe7b2ccc`. Local `main` and the combat branch pointed at that commit before AR01. The terrain branch is in its history. The local `architecture/pre-ar01` tag marks the same verified version. AR02 starts from the tested AR01 graft extraction at `acf8f724a437a0330e349da9704ede2c9290c426`. AR03 starts from the card ledger at `83e67e255830e64bad72d33de2071d1636a3a483`. AR04 starts from the expedition wallet at `6212a30fed1ced6cc4e3adf603fdecbbc4bfd41a`. AR05 starts from the departure settlement extraction at `fddb1fc5cd63bac41361b9caefb513c2e497108a`. AR06 starts from monster reward receipt ownership at `a287458d884f929abd4f6f50fa0b10bf9917d18b`. AR07 starts from native cooldown ownership at `54b1c5b1a6a647139171b4cb9d0c2f2182fc0b85`. AR08 starts from player health ownership at `72389e59066dcc3d63d09da5c4e35058e135cbf5`. AR09 starts from player lifecycle ownership at `956f8302d4dfcaea5210e1c355d8eb986fbe5b0e`. AR10 starts from encounter roster ownership at `92c920cbe97bd623f5baccf6021a578c8c40cf74`. AR11 starts from enemy tactics and behavior ownership at `383251986350fe8e3c809bcc8c94717b49022b97`. AR12 starts from ordinary enemy attack execution at `c0c147471530bd8358f770ecd17da2a2a961df67`. AR13 starts from incoming player hit resolution at `5fb00ae287120518d0b28493e510c9dbfb29b870`, preserved by the local `architecture/pre-ar13` recovery tag. AR14 starts from expedition participation ownership at `c436378fd86ec01674c9abb02284fb8ff8c122fd`, preserved by `architecture/pre-ar14`.

[The title](../main_menu.gd) admits a player and selects solo, host or join networking. [The world scene](../world.tscn) instantiates [Game](../game.gd) and [Stage](../stage.gd). Game accepts inputs and RPCs and coordinates gameplay and saves. Stage presents the world. The child `GameplayUI` scene owns the HUD and overlay shell. Several service scripts still use Game as their data store.

The sole gameplay contract is now `modern-gameplay-1`: affinity-based Core/Form/Catalyst combat, free checkpoint cards, cumulative expedition grafts, and five connected class trees. There is no selectable historical gameplay fallback. `architecture/pre-modern-cutover` preserves the verified AR14 parent (`12c13c888621dd57e944088986b76bdbfad7cf41`). Git history and protected tags provide recovery.

The modern catalog on `main` contains **137 cards, including 12 Unique acquisitions**, across all nine affinities. Edit card requirements and provisional contributions in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`. The CE02–CE04 sections below record the implemented effects and verification. Locked expedition loadouts, tag and capability eligibility, scaling and transformation design are defined in the [approved card catalog and progression design](COMBAT_BALANCE_MILESTONES.md#approved-card-and-progression-design).

### Editing shared runtime behavior

- Card loadout predicates live at the top of `affinity_card_catalog.gd`. The ledger resolves eligibility facts once per offer scan and checks the selected card against the current build again at purchase. Shared requirements keep the same card IDs, values and capability checks.
- `enemy_affinity_effects.gd` gives affinity handlers access to the effects on the current enemy. The roster and leader tactics retain membership and movement. `outgoing_hits.gd::affinity_and_clear` applies a resolved individual affinity hit and checks clearance. Batch effects use `affinity_world` and check clearance after the batch. `EnemyCatalog.movement_speed` supplies the shared movement modifiers for the Wilds and arena.
- `garden_growth_state.gd::NETWORK_VERSION` covers the meaning and acknowledgement of replicated Garden events. Both peers verify it through `network_contract.gd` before character registration. Expansion playback requires version 2. The transport and compact snapshot remain protocol 41.
- Guarded simulation reuses a lease check for up to one second. Durable operations still check ownership under the transaction lock. `game_transaction.gd::save_growth_checkpoint` captures the Garden fields the timer can change. Cross-system actions keep their wider rollback snapshot.

## Expedition results (ER01)

`expedition_statistics.gd` owns in-memory counters by host session, participation run ID and reward-character identity. `expedition_transitions.gd` admits a row after loadout and reward admission, advances its endless floor number, and freezes it before actor cleanup. The summary measures wall-clock expedition time through the viewer's departure and counts distinct committed encounter clears. An early return captures teammates at that point with their current participation status. Simultaneous returns share the final party standings.

| Display | Counting boundary |
|---|---|
| Damage dealt and affinity damage | `outgoing_hit_context.gd` records HP actually lost after armor, capped at remaining enemy HP. Bleed weights each contributor's accumulated potency, Burning uses live stack counts, and Brittle uses the three frozen shatter numerators. Fractional HP credit is retained. Each Surge wave already carries one source's resolved share. Bloodletting, Hemorrhagic Rupture, Shatterwave and Flashover have separate labels. Other hits contribute to total damage. |
| Defeats | `game.gd::record_enemy_death` counts a confirmed roster death once, including summons. The source of the lethal contact gets individual credit, including the combat resolver's chosen source of a shared tick. Gate elites and boss leaders are subsets. A death without a known finisher still counts for the party. |
| Damage taken and prevented | `incoming_player_hit.gd` observes accepted enemy contacts. Prevention is the difference between the HP the incoming contact could have removed and the HP loss after the existing defenses, each bounded by available health. It belongs to the protected character. The resolver cannot always identify an individual Ward or Bark provider. Misses, grace, and resource spending are excluded. |
| Effective healing, falls and revives | `expedition_sustain.gd` and `vital_combat.gd` measure the real HP increase. A strongest Vital regeneration tie between providers stays in the unattributed party total. Floor recovery, changes to HP capacity and the revival refill are excluded. `expedition_life.gd` counts successful falls and credits the ally who completed a revival. |
| Class XP, cards and grafts | Class XP is the gross amount on newly admitted reward receipts, before profile limits or forfeiture. It is independent of personal kills. Only an accepted card copy or purchased graft successor counts. Opening, skipping and rerolling offers add zero. |

For the ordinary save route, the host’s Save Garden command or a new registration can finish a confirmed commit’s pending cleanup. The persistence session verifies the same journal and participant bytes under the existing legacy gate before releasing departure and reward holds. Cleanup does not execute the departure again. Uncertainty requires reopening and reconciling the save. The disposable `legacy_departure_outcomes` fixture covers locked cleanup, verified rollback, uncertainty, title handoff and fresh-process recovery. `legacy_departure_rejoin` covers an independent guest’s disconnect, settlement and readmission.

Both gameplay transaction paths include statistics in their in-memory savepoints. Confirmed rollback restores the counters and discards tentative results. Commit and committed cleanup-pending permit presentation. A disconnect freezes the row immediately, and confirmed departure settlement makes the report available to the same character on Garden readmission. The host retains up to four completed reports. Ending a host process loses these records and any uncommitted results. The detailed reports are transient. LA01 aggregates their confirmed outcomes within the existing settlement writers.

`game.gd::receive_expedition_result` is a reliable, authority-only RPC carrying either an expedition-entry marker or a frozen result. Admission binding, character identity and ordered run IDs protect delivery. Both peers need the ER01 RPC contract. The protocol-41 combat packet layout is unchanged. `ui/gameplay/expedition_summary.gd` presents the overview and one tab per participant. The gameplay overlay and a voluntary return to title reuse the same view. Spectator focus preserves an open finalized report when waiting-state vitals arrive ahead of Garden membership. Closing the application ends the opportunity to view an in-memory result.

Focused coverage lives in `tests/expedition_statistics.gd`, `tests/shared_affinity_status.gd`, `tests/guarded_expedition.gd`, `tests/expedition_forfeiture_coop.gd`, `tests/expedition_results_rejoin.gd`, `tests/four_players_coop.gd` and `tests/network_contract.gd`.

## Living Archive (LA01)

The starting Archive station offers The Garden and My Character scopes with Botanical, Expeditions, Combat and Garden Development tabs. Its records have no progression consumers. Open it at the physical station. The host answers a proximity- and identity-checked read, sending the Garden and only the asking character’s slice. Reliable responses bind to the current connection and character. While the panel is open, an unchanged digest avoids another payload. Archive data travels outside combat snapshots.

| Record | Source and meaning |
|---|---|
| Botanical collection | The existing species discovery ledger and current altar catalog supply completion denominators. Distinct-parent material recipes are synthesis and matching ordinary parts are refinement. Recorded successful seed recipes removed by WF02 appear separately. A later failed attempt retains the historical success flag while using the current salvage outcome. |
| Botanical activity | A mature crop counts once at accepted harvest. Propagation counts every seed delivered, ordinary parts count actual yield, and a refined part counts only if awarded. Successful repeat seed combinations count as activity. The Wilds adds one ER01 observation per accepted personal specimen claim, independent of bundle size or eventual loot forfeiture. |
| Expedition | ER01 captures each character’s own elapsed time, deepest reached floor and highest actual clear before departure. The Garden adds wall time only as a run advances beyond its previous settled duration. Combined character participation adds the individual times. Each distinct run counts once. At least one extraction marks a successful Garden run. A wipe of the remaining party can coexist with an earlier extraction. Returning Root deposits count individual secured units immediately. |
| Combat | Applied HP, lethal-source defeats, effective healing, protected-recipient prevention, revivals, falls and affinity subsets use the ER01 definitions above. Party damage, healing, deaths and affinity totals include unattributed outcomes. A returning character’s private row adds only their own attributed contributions. |
| Garden development | Read lifetime and spendable Knowledge, research completion, purchased frontiers and reclaimed cells, owned beds and cultivation structures, placed/stored counts and the current Heart stage from the existing owners. Lifetime paid builds count at purchase. Lifetime contribution Energy counts the accepted Heart quote. Refunds, redeployment and starting grants do not increment either counter. |

**Durability.** The Garden serializer owns one version-1 Archive record with a stable Garden identity, shared totals and compact rows by persistent character identity. The gameplay transaction records both sides of the activity together. On departure, the existing return UUID and member receipt pair the individual row with the positive changes in cumulative party totals. A later settlement of an older disconnected participant uses the frozen result and its original UUID. After all rows close, the retained history needs only a completion marker. The existing 32-run return history bounds outstanding identifiers. Detailed reports and attack events have no permanent Archive representation. Rollback restores counters, receipts and ER01 together. Confirmed cleanup-pending keeps the records. Uncertain durability holds publication and replay for recovery.

**Character travel.** The selected local profile keeps one cumulative slice per visited, verified Garden. Within the current Garden its authoritative row supersedes that Garden’s local copy. Across Gardens, counters add and personal bests use the maximum. The host never incorporates a joining client’s claimed totals into the Garden. Guarded guests receive absolute host-issued slices through the existing revision/digest and acknowledgement path, including readmission following a disconnect. Older unguarded guest sessions lack origin-bound profile receipts. Those visits remain readable from their host Garden. A Garden-only write can precede the portable mirror; a later authorized profile projection or clean local exit catches up without adding a delta or replaying gameplay.

Existing discoveries, recipes, guardian memories, decoded flags, return discoveries, campaign bests and physical development are visible immediately. If a historical clear exceeds the deepest encounter observed by LA01, the station labels the reached depth as a lower bound. New activity counters start at zero on old saves. A crash before run settlement can lose the unfinished run’s in-memory ER01 activity. The existing save/recovery policy governs that boundary.

## Shared affinity status rules

Status contributions remain in each existing enemy body, not a second roster. `affinity_status_source.gd` supplies captured actor/connection/life/floor identity, target validation, fixed-point HP and deterministic aggregate-hit credit. Family handlers retain their own formulas. Source slots cooperate; an activation is a deduplication identity, not a separate DOT clock. Existing OutgoingHits/EncounterRoster/MonsterRewards remain the damage/death/reward authorities; generated damage stays secondary.

| Family | Current shared rules and authoritative tuning |
|---|---|
| Thorn | `thorn_affinity.gd`: ordinary/refined personal cap **12/18 HP per tick** before card contributions; strongest matching equipped base counts once, matching slot cap upgrades add. Core/Catalyst add **25%**, Form **10%**, at most once per **1,000 ms per target/player/slot** while in the relevant patch. One aggregate **900 ms** cadence, fractional HP carry. Each contributor expires after **4,500 ms** without application; its cap is counted once. A stronger accepted cap can increase but is not downgraded by weaker applications during that maintained contribution. Expiry, life/connection change and floor departure remove it. Damage credit selects the largest contribution, then lowest peer/binding. The old independent slot DOT clocks are retired. Sawtooth Briars retains its ID, rarity values and acquired copies, now explicitly raising this personal cap. |
| Frost | `frost_balance.gd`: **12/21 HP** ordinary/refined maximum shatter. Each stack captures one third of the applying player's current maximum; sum the three exact numerators and round once, half upward. Oldest three accepted stacks rupture; surplus starts the next sequence. **8%/13%** per-stack slowing, bosses half; **3,600 ms** shared idle expiry. Rupture has **no baseline root**. Native roots from other mechanics remain unchanged. Third-application actor receives rupture credit; every stack retains its source facts. |
| Fire | `fire_combat.gd`: one shared intensity, **0.125 HP per stack per 250 ms tick**. **No fixed stack ceiling**. Before a same-time tick, each **500 ms** decay removes **10%** of current stacks rounded up, or **8** stacks if larger, never more than remain. Loss is approximately linear in intensity: stronger maintained application sustains proportionally greater intensity rather than hitting a universal DPS ceiling. Core/Form/Catalyst add **16/8/12 stacks**, refined adds **4** before scoped cards. Source credit expires after **3,000 ms** idle. First admission ticks immediately; later applications keep one cadence. Ember Wake adds stacks at most once per **750 ms per cast/enemy**. Integer fractional HP carries between ticks. Decay works per contributor, not per individual stack. |
| Water | `water_combat.gd` / `water_tidal_return.gd`: strongest **18%/30%** Drenched reduction, maximum **30%**, independently expiring **4,200 ms** sources. Core/Form/Catalyst add **40/15/30 pressure**, refined +10. FIFO captured contributions spend **100**, preserving overflow. Tidal Return splits one **6 damage / 6 healing** baseline Surge into player-originating weighted outgoing and gentler returning waves. Direct damage, healing, ordinary Slow cleanse and native push are distinct; secondary waves create no pressure. See CE03 for duration/geometry, attribution, cancellation and provisional card tuning. |
| Wood | `wood_affinity.gd`: **no 48-point pool ceiling**. Recipient capacity is the sum of full, unspent, independently expiring grants; absorption remains **40%** of an otherwise unblocked hit, limited by remaining capacity. Core **30/40** for **6,000 ms**, Catalyst **12/18** for **4,200 ms**, before existing duration cards. Earliest expiry is consumed first, ties by grant order. Source identity, one-grant admission, burst weighting and recipient burst interval remain intact. Form remains Splintering Strikes. Capacity can increase without becoming whole-hit protection. |
| Vital | `vital_combat.gd`: strongest live capacity **12/18 HP** and regeneration **3/5 HP/s** independently, one **1,000 ms** regeneration cadence. Core/Form/Catalyst durations stay **5,500/2,800/4,000 ms** before existing cards. Different source magnitudes retain their own expiry even within one provider/slot. Recipient life/floor changes invalidate old grants; accepted short-lived ally grants may finish after provider loss. Capacity increases never heal; decreases clamp current HP. |
| Harvest | `PlayerVitals.Health.spent` is the immutable resource operation, never an incoming hit. The acquired CE03 toggle spends up to **2 filled bonus HP per accepted ordinary launch**, for **2 damage per HP actually spent** on its first eligible direct target. Normal HP and maximum capacity are unchanged. Empty reserves suspend the bonus while preserving the toggle. The pure full-reserve quote retains its earlier configurable foundation defaults; live Harvest tuning is solely in its catalog definition. Deep Roots is unimplemented. |

`affinity_cards.gd::affinity_values` is the pure numerical contribution path. `ResolvedCombatBuild.Snapshot.affinity_potency` and its detached `affinity_build` inspection expose it; `combat_action_events.gd::begin` captures those same values at action launch. Native wire facts retain their original material baselines, so host/guest reconstruction does not double-apply cards. Equipment descriptions use the resolved numbers, including cross-slot Bleed/shatter potency and acquired duration/regen contributions. Past stacks do not acquire later upgrades. Already accepted DOTs may finish while a source is downed in the same life; revival or connection loss invalidates that source. Enemy HP, recipient HP, cards and grafts retain their existing owners. `combat_ruleset.gd::SHARED_STATUS_VERSION` deliberately enters the existing definition fingerprint; older gameplay builds are refused while protocol-41 serialization stays unchanged.

`tests/shared_affinity_status.gd` covers captured mixed-source rupture, fractional rounding, all-slot Bleed cooperation, four contributor caps, shared cadence, decay, repeated Surges/overflow, recipient protection, Harvest resource quotes and stale-life/connection cleanup. `tests/shared_affinity_party.gd` reuses the guarded title/Garden/Wilds and RPC fixture with Warden/Weaver/Ranger/Reaper participants. Its focused helper is `tests/support/shared_affinity_network_case.gd`; runtime equipment changes there are explicit fixture setup. Four native casts produce one 60-HP Bleed ceiling and one 72-stack Burning count; two four-player Water rounds produce exactly three Surges and 60 overflow. Native Ironbark/Vital Bloom, actual damage/deaths/rewards, floor advancement, extraction and fifth-client refusal complete through existing operations. Two-player Vital and Wood fixtures also verify Garden rejoin. This is automated local loopback, not separate-PC or internet verification.

The pre-scaling checkpoint focused selection passed **23 suites** (nine family controls, cards, outgoing hits, protection/conditions, guarded lifecycle, Reaper/Oracle, status presentation, roster, shared-status unit/party, two rejoin pairs and incompatible-definition refusal). **106 validation-tool self-tests** pass. Exact-input results are reused; original failures remain in the ignored validation evidence. Accepted source evidence: `.godot/validation/final-four-affinity-final-accepted-20261120-061645/summary.json`. Tool evidence: `.godot/validation/final-four-step1-tools-20261120-061649`. That checkpoint grew the registry **239 to 241** for the two new shared-status fixtures. Common combat-contract changes conservatively stale broader evidence; no broad historical/release refresh or export was performed.

The full-occupancy four-client probe uses **20 scouts** and ongoing shared Burning: **1,196-byte** fast frames, 132 successful sends, median recorded encoding **246.5 microseconds** (max 348), zero observed sequence gaps/stale rejects, guest maximum apply gaps **52.6–59.0 ms**. Existing isolated CPU samples over 150 simulated steps: four players/12 enemies with Fire/Water/Vital, **212.46 microseconds/step**; four players/12 scouts plus leader, four Thorn fields and a Bark absorption plan, **994.86 microseconds/step**. These are effect/encoding samples, not graphical full-frame or internet latency claims.

One integration correction preserves the existing encounter authority: `EncounterRoster.register_leader_tactics` attaches a role body to the same enemy without replacing its HP, defeat state or spawn identity. This prevents lazy elite initialization from invalidating an accepted status. No enemy AI decisions/interfaces or Neural Arena implementation changed. The roster and shared-status units cover this case. The Void displacement fixture's manually constructed Fire field now includes its per-target stack-application gate; gameplay displacement remains unchanged.

The two existing Fire cards retain identities, rarity values, limits and per-copy ownership; their described consumer now adds stacks, and the startup catalog validator explicitly accepts stack units. Sawtooth Briars describes its personal Bleed cap. The catalog stayed at **28 cards** at that shared-mechanics checkpoint; CE02/CE03 now expand it to 101. `combat_status.gd` coalesces Burning-count and Saturation projections to **250 ms** through existing status events/recovery; `status_feedback.gd` displays the numbers. No fast-frame fields, protocol version, RPCs or transport cadence changed. The rules fingerprint intentionally rejects older status semantics. CE03 now implements the optional Harvest toggle and once-per-launch bonus-HP expenditure; Deep Roots remains unimplemented. Production persistence remains disabled; all checks used disposable storage.


### Affinity scaling policy and limits

A normal build must function; exceptional investment may become exceptionally powerful. No automatic damage equalization or aggressive diminishing returns are introduced. Baselines live in each family's `QUALITY`/named constants. Derived results stay read-only values; existing enemy/recipient records, health values, ledgers and host authority retain ownership.

| Value | Classification and editing rule |
|---|---|
| Thorn 12/18 | **Material baseline**, not an absolute maximum. `affinity_values` uses the strongest matching equipped base once and adds each explicitly scoped card contribution once. Accepted cap increases require new eligible applications to build damage; old accumulated potency is not promoted for free. No separate absolute potency ceiling. |
| Frost 12/21; three stacks | 12/21 are **baseline shatter potencies**. Three is a **mechanical rupture threshold**. Each stack freezes one third of its source potency; final rounding occurs once. Higher numerical potency remains effective, without baseline root. |
| Fire applications and decay | Stack applications are **baseline values plus actual build contributions**. The former 96 limit is retired. Current decay is an ongoing resource loss, not a new admission cap. Current source duration and Core field application gate remain explicit mechanical rules. |
| Water 100; 6 damage/2.4 m | 100 is a **mechanical threshold**. Damage/radius/application amounts are **baseline plus authored modifier inputs**; overflow is not clipped. The 30% defensive reduction remains the explicitly bounded strongest-source policy. |
| Wood 30/40 Core, 12/18 Catalyst | **Baseline grant amounts**. The resource pool is the sum of valid grants, not a fixed 48 ceiling. Duration and partial consumption constrain endurance. A higher pool cannot increase the 40% absorption fraction or grant invulnerability. |
| Vital 12/18, 3/5 HP/s | **Baseline capacity/regen**, with strongest live values resolved independently. Current Greening Chime copies add actual regeneration. Non-Vital capacity remains separate; growth never fills HP. Harvest is a resource quote, retains capacity, and has no activation control or new Unique card. |
| Other limits | **12 card acquisitions**, individual existing card copy policies, **six graft purchases**, native graft reinforcement limits, weapon/dodge timing rules, active-field counts and event admission budgets are unchanged. They are separate content/mechanical/technical policies, not replacement affinity potency ceilings. |
| Numeric representation | Newly uncapped Burning/Bark stores reject unrepresentable integer arithmetic instead of wrapping or clipping. Burning reserves fractional carry before milli-HP multiplication and computes percentage decay without overflowing `count * percentage`. Network HP fields still use the existing signed 32-bit protocol representation. No new gameplay ceiling or protocol layout is introduced. |

At the scaling-foundation checkpoint, the original 28-card catalog had no shatter-potency, Bark-capacity, Saturation-application, Surge-strength/radius or Vital-capacity card. Tests at that checkpoint supplied those inputs as fixtures. The later CE02–CE04 entries document the cards that now use them in ordinary play.

| Focused stationary-contact comparison | Baseline | Specialized / cooperative |
|---|---|---|
| Refined Thorn tick cap | 18, approximately 20 pre-mitigation DPS when filled | Four Epic Sawtooth Briars copies: 34, approximately 37.8 DPS; four such players share a 136 cap, approximately 151.1 DPS. Buildup is still required. |
| Fire Form only, refined Vine Bow | 12 stacks/contact, 850 ms interval; **3.5 DOT DPS** over the last 10 s of a 30 s simulation | Twelve current cards (four Epic Hotter Embers, Heart of Cinders and Light Grip): 28 Form stacks/contact at 590 ms, **106.8 DOT DPS**, peak 244 stacks. Four specialists: **434.6 DOT DPS**, peak 990. Heart of Cinders is owned but not activated in this Form-only measurement; native attacks/Core/class damage are excluded. |
| Wood, 100 HP incoming hit | Ordinary solo Core: 30 capacity, absorbs 30 then empties | Refined Core/Catalyst plus actual duration cards allow an eight-second Core recast to overlap for 98 capacity. Absorbs 40, leaves 58; independent expiry remains. Four native mixed-quality Core grants give 140, not 48. |
| Vital regeneration | Refined 18 temporary HP and 5 HP/s | Four Epic Greening Chime copies: 21 HP/s. Six real health-graft receipts at floors 8–48 and four Hardy Frame copies add 66 separate non-Vital HP. No free healing or recursive capacity calculation. |

`tests/affinity_scaling.gd` verifies these actual definitions/budgets, frozen Frost contributions, stronger/weak-source Water expiry and overflow, future potency inputs, inactive/re-activated equipment cards, guest reconstruction, integer rejection, and life/floor cleanup. Other current family, defense, card, UI, class and rollback fixtures remain maintained. The four-client native-cast fixture also verifies host **and all three guests** observing shared Burning above 96. The existing controlled recovery fixture sizes its two nonlethal hits from the actual Bark pool, rather than assuming the retired cap; one break and current HP/status recovery must still be observed. Registry count is **242**; broad release evidence refresh remains a separate task.

Current scaling acceptance: **25 focused suites and 106 validation-tool self-tests pass**, using disposable save roots. Accepted exact-input evidence is consolidated in `.godot/validation/final-four-scaling-accepted-20261120-100152/summary.json`; tool evidence is `.godot/validation/final-four-step1-tools-20261120-100153/summary.json`. Independent two- and four-player loopback verifies casts, additive Bark, all three guests observing Burning above 96, current-state recovery, floor travel, extraction/Garden rejoin and fifth-client refusal. The compact frame remains **1,196 bytes**. Definition mismatch is refused with zero guest admissions. This is automated local evidence, not manual balance approval or separate-computer/internet testing. Earlier failing fixtures/logs remain preserved; their stale capped expectations were corrected rather than weakening runtime contributions. No broad release evidence refresh was run.

Balance concerns remain explicit: the minimum eight-stack decay creates a meaningful maintenance breakpoint, and the reported Fire comparison is affinity DOT on stationary contacts, not whole-build combat DPS or a manual balance verdict. Water still resolves one pulse per completed threshold; currently authored inputs are bounded by the existing actions/catalog. Extremely large future Saturation grants need a focused execution-cost review before authoring, rather than introducing a hidden potency cap here. No Harvest input, Deep Roots card, catalog expansion, enemy decision change, save activation or export is part of this milestone.

## Card eligibility (CE01)

CE01 was implemented on `combat/ce01-card-eligibility`, consolidated at `ab2e82ee8c811acaff00ac55353202190ee03502` from the affinity-scaling implementation `12d54b6251db5917cd67741890f2f01fcd5c24cb` and the focused documentation cherry-pick of `c2fc7415603fb8b5b7863749a165006a3637cb9c`. Local `main` remains `e1265e0dd750c4b1d63382921d574ccf11fd0562`. Existing branches/tags and stashed references are preserved; no main integration is implied.

| Editing location / owner | Current boundary |
|---|---|
| `affinity_card_catalog.gd` | All 28 searchable displayed names, IDs, values, scopes and policies remain here. Each inline `requires` expression uses the same small model. Only the two already implemented Uniques declare `grants`; no transformation is added. |
| `card_requirements.gd` | Pure predicate validation, deterministic refusal and matching over detached facts: `all`, `any`, exact class/focus/weapon/node, `plant`, `tags_all`/`tags_any`, `distinct_slots`, `capability`, `owned`. A plant predicate binds one plant; distinct-slot matching cannot reuse it. Unknown/malformed predicates fail closed, including invalid alternatives. |
| `card_eligibility.gd` | Derives fact/ID views from existing class, plant, skill, Form, Catalyst and graft definitions. No Game/actor or authoritative acquisition state is stored. Native action/response IDs identify executable capabilities; botanical identities alone never grant mechanics. Catalog dependency validation rejects cycles and unsupported grants. |
| `affinity_card_ledger.gd` | Sole card owner, still controlling cached offers, revisions, acquisition history and independent copies. `counts` is derived from its receipts. `requirement` delegates to the shared predicate path; copy/Unique limits and existing numerical headroom remain separate mandatory gates. |
| Existing effect consumers | `water_combat.gd` and `affinity_cards.gd` advertise `CARD_GRANTS` for the same parameter/method their real Dew Shelter and Echoing Growth execution reads. This is verified consumer metadata, not a second manually authored capability registry. Unsupported Harvest/Splinterburst/Mirrorstep declarations cannot validate. |
| Build/graft projections | `AffinityBuild` projects the actual selected path; resolved snapshots project card counts, and `ExpeditionGrafts.resolution` derives purchase counts from the same graft receipts. These detached values do not own or independently mutate progression/acquisitions. |

There are exactly two plant-tag roles: `primary_affinity` and unordered `supporting_affinities`; existing definitions are unchanged. Capability selectors distinguish producer and slot. `target_scope` means the native adapter's target footprint, while explicit grant `recipient` describes its actual destination; an enemy-contact trigger is not automatically the recipient of self-Bark. Fine-grained future resource/action capabilities require actual consumer contracts, not inferred tag aliases.

Loadout and selected nodes remain locked by existing Garden/travel permissions. Earned XP does not select nodes. Offer facts do not inspect current HP, Bark quantity, cooldown readiness or transient enemy statuses. Acquired card/graft revisions supply evolving prerequisites. Ordinary numerical effects still use the same resolved build and proc paths, including cooperative shared Bleed/Burning, and preserve all values and copy limits.

Short offers contain only available distinct identities. Rerolls may replace a short offer with a shorter valid one but must add a genuinely different identity; no rarity-only rerolls. A refused replacement creates neither a successor ledger nor a debit intent. Cached reopening is unchanged; selection rechecks current facts/headroom. Game retains its existing host authorization and guarded wallet/card transaction coordination without a new card manager.

The fast protocol-41 layout, RPCs and persistence settings are unchanged. Derived build fields use existing reliable detail containers. The requirement schema version joins the existing gameplay-definition fingerprint so mixed eligibility/content builds cannot silently join.

Focused ownership is in `tests/card_eligibility.gd`: **662 checks** include a frozen baseline oracle for all 28 titles/scopes/parameters/rarity values/policies, 405 real class/focus/weapon/family combinations, tag topology, actual capabilities, acquired counts, verified grants, cycles, headroom and zero-to-three-card offers. Existing EC01, resolved-build/UI, graft and guarded transaction fixtures retain actual combat and rollback coverage.

CE01 verification: **9 baseline suites; 29 current-input final suites / 39 process verdicts; 106 validation-tool self-tests**, all passed with isolated disposable saves. Final coverage includes all five class trees, all nine affinity controls, independent two-player matching/refused-build checks, and four-player card/graft loopbacks with fifth-player refusal. This verifies local automated loopback, not separate-computer/internet multiplayer or a manual balance verdict. Evidence: `.godot/validation/final-four-ce01-resumed-20261120-173129/summary.json` and `.godot/validation/final-four-step1-tools-20261120-173341/summary.json`. After the app-update interruption, the final summary retained 16 completed receipts only after checking exact current keys and original log hashes; the remaining 13 suites executed normally. The registry increased from 242 to 243 suites. Shared infrastructure/definition changes conservatively invalidate older evidence across all 243; a broad release-evidence refresh is deferred, not claimed complete. No release export or real-save write was performed.

## First affinity card expansion (CE02)

Development branch: `combat/ce02-affinity-cards`, based on accepted CE01 `e49163e9f357f77d9df738010b358982b771a98b`. Main remains unchanged. The families are implemented and verified sequentially; unlisted families below are not claimed complete.

- **Thorn: implemented, 11 additions; 39 total cards at the Thorn checkpoint.** Definitions and all rarity contributions remain searchable in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`. New `family`/`affinity` scope applies only through a functioning matching source. Personal-cap additions count once, while slot application bonuses remain scoped. `thorn_affinity.gd` owns direct buildup, same-target sequences, opening applications, short-lived next-attack preparation and the one-per-death burst claim. Generated card damage carries explicit secondary lineage through `outgoing_hits.gd` and cannot chain death bursts.
- `card_active_actions.gd` extends the existing reliable `garden_action` transport for acquired actions. It validates session binding, character/life/world stamp, acquisition, prerequisites, aim, targets, cooldown and request sequence on the host. It holds no card ownership. Cooldowns/preparations are run-only actor state; identity changes invalidate them, and `AffinityCards.clear` retires them. Reliable owner details provide skill-bar state without changing protocol 41's compact layout. Four configurable additional input slots coexist with B/Q/R; only Bloodletting is implemented here.
- Thorn provisional values (Common/Uncommon/Rare/Epic): Density and Crimson +5/8/11/14 application percentage points; coverage +0.20/0.30/0.40/0.50 m per side; Serrated and successive Relentless increments +2/3/4/5 points; Opening +5/8/11/14 points; Momentum +3/5/7/9 direct HP within 4 s; Sanguine +2/3/4/5 personal tick-cap HP; Unclosing +250/400/550/700 ms; Hemorrhagic +50/75/100/125% of accumulated tick potency. Hemorrhagic requires 50% buildup, uses one strongest eligible owner and 3 m clear-line area, spreads no Bleed, and claims before damage callbacks. Bloodletting is Unique: 200% accumulated tick damage, 8 m combat sight, 8 s cooldown, no Bleed consumption. These are provisional tuning values, not permanent balance.


- **Frost: implemented, 13 additions; 52 total cards at this checkpoint.** `frost_combat.gd` freezes each source-weighted numerator and the acquired rupture modifiers on its stack. Core/Form/Catalyst-specific potency is separate from the one global potency addition. Returning Winter adds closing stacks; Shatterwave emits immediate secondary damage; Avalanche propagates attributed stacks. They are independent acquisitions and can coexist. Propagated ruptures retain their native weighted damage and acquired Rime Lock/Shatterwave; only another Avalanche propagation is suppressed. Wave damage applies no Brittle, so it cannot recursively rupture. Later genuine applications can activate new ruptures normally. Native class slot metadata enables Shatter Rhythm without changing old adapters that do not provide a slot.
- Frost provisional values (Common/Uncommon/Rare/Epic): source-specific shatter potency +3/5/7/9 HP (Core, Form and Catalyst separately); Returning Winter +1/2/3/4 closing stacks; Relentless Chill +0.250/0.400/0.550/0.700 stack credit per eligible hit; Cold Precision +1/2/3/4 direct HP per pre-hit stack; Spreading Chill 10/15/20/25% potency on one neighboring stack within 2.4 m, once per action; Shatter Rhythm +2/3/4/5 potency to the same class slot's next eligible use within 5 s; Crystalline +3/5/7/9 global potency; Deep Freeze +1/2/3/4 Slow percentage points per stack; Rime Lock +150/250/350/450 ms root; Shatterwave +20/30/40/50% rupture damage within 2.4 m; Avalanche is Unique, one captured stack to other enemies within 2.4 m. First/second Slow progression and the three-stack threshold are unchanged; no card means no root.


- **Fire: implemented, 10 additions; final catalog 62 cards (57 numerical, five Unique).** `fire_combat.gd` owns consecutive-hit preparation, contributed stack retention, target-global Flashover spending and frozen periodic Wildfire plans. `weapon_attack_world.gd` and the pure Stats projection share its interval calculation; temporary speed never changes Q/R timing. A permanently capped basic attack cannot receive an ineffective Feverish Tempo offer. All 28 original definitions, requirements, rarity values and playtest acquisition/reroll settings remain identical to CE01.

| New Fire card | Provisional C / U / R / E contribution | Actual rule / units |
|---|---|---|
| Kindled Roots | 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 | Additional stacks per Core application, including existing field maintenance. |
| Lingering Coals | 250 / 400 / 550 / 700 ms | Trail lifetime; actual cast expiry and visual lifetime agree. |
| Stoking Strikes | 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 | Additional stacks per successive attack after the first; sequence resets after 3 s without an eligible hit. |
| Feverish Tempo | 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 percentage points | Speed per sequence step on already-Burning victims, lasting 2 s. Uses the existing 230 ms attack interval limit; extra copies improve the ramp. |
| Incendiary Followthrough | 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 | Extra Catalyst stacks if the enemy was already Burning before application. |
| Ember Resonance | 10 / 15 / 20 / 25% | Once per class action, apply that share of its offered stacks to other enemies within 2.4 m and clear line; leaf applications produce no recursive procs. |
| Banked Heat | 10 / 15 / 20 / 25 efficiency | Divide that owner's assigned decay by `1 + efficiency/100`; carry fractional stack loss. No zero-value rounding at low intensity, no fixed stack ceiling. |
| Feeding the Flames | 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 | Additional stacks per eligible application in every functioning Fire slot. |
| Flashover | 50 / 75 / 100 / 125% | Damage in HP per stack actually consumed. On its owner's native application at 8+ intensity, spend 25%, retain at least one stack, and deal area damage within 2.4 m. One global 2 s trigger reservation per target. |
| Wildfire — Unique | Fixed unlock | At 8+ intensity, spread 50% of the shared stacks to enemies within 2.4 m, once per second. Choose one eligible owning contributor deterministically. Propagated-only contributions cannot forward the effect. |

The eight-stack trigger thresholds and 50% Wildfire spread are **provisional**, selected so an ordinary eight-stack Form application can supply useful special effects without first acquiring another upgrade. They are thresholds, not intensity ceilings. Fire retains its 250 ms damage tick, 500 ms decay clock and independently attributed contributions. Flashover consumes stacks; Wildfire copies stacks and preserves the original. Target reservations explicitly bind world and spawn generation. Secondary-only Burning cannot start further card death bursts; legitimate later native input can enable future spread.

All three transformations coexist on a verified eight-card locked build (Thorn Core / Frost Form / Fire Catalyst). Bloodletting consumes no shared Bleed. Avalanche leaf ruptures retain acquired Rime Lock and Shatterwave, while another Avalanche propagation is suppressed. Shatterwave damage applies no new Brittle. This preserves powerful finite combinations without recursive spread chains. All 31 new numerical cards have authored contribution labels beside their values; no engine parameter names are exposed as preview labels.

Final CE02 evidence: **20 focused gameplay/network suites / 37 process verdicts**, all passed with stable inputs, in `.godot/validation/final-four-ce02-commit-20261120-200745/summary.json`. This includes all three families' independent two- and four-player episodes, fifth-player refusal, exactly-once action requests, floor/extraction cleanup, existing card effects, shared-status scaling, original Fire behavior, Stats, modern-only compatibility and guarded rollback. A subsequent label/description-only presentation edit left effects, requirements and numbers unchanged; six current-input checks (Fire interactions and every new rarity preview, CE01 predicates, all original card consumers, content, matching/refused builds) passed in `.godot/validation/final-four-ce02-presentation-20261120-201157/summary.json`. Validation-tool self-tests: **106 passed**, `.godot/validation/final-four-step1-tools-20261120-201348/summary.json`. These are local automated checks; separate-computer/internet multiplayer, manual balance and release packaging remain unverified. Registry: 252 suites. Broader source/release evidence is deliberately not refreshed. Main, protected saves, persistence cutover settings, neural decision interfaces and compact protocol 41 remain unchanged.

Live tuning is authoritative in `affinity_card_catalog.gd` under the displayed card name (`values`, units, `label`, `tuning`, requirements and real consumer). The numbers above record this provisional checkpoint; change the definition, then maintain this reference as tuning changes. No other affinity transformation, new graft or card family is implemented by CE02.

Focused Frost evidence: **ten suites passed** with stable inputs in `.godot/validation/final-four-ce02-frost-verified-20261120-194447/summary.json`: Frost and Thorn card mechanics, CE01 predicates, original 28 consumers, shared statuses, original Frost/Ward compatibility, content, guarded rollback, and independent two/four-player loopback with overflow refusal. Earlier failures exposed an optional adapter slot and premature fixture damage observation; both were corrected before this passing run. These are automated local checks, not manual balance or internet multiplayer acceptance.

Focused Thorn evidence: **eight focused suites passed**, with stable current inputs, in `.godot/validation/final-four-ce02-thorn-commit-20261120-193423/summary.json`. Coverage includes real two- and four-player local loopback, extra-action RPCs, duplicate requests, normal floor/extraction flow, fifth-player refusal, shared statuses, all original card consumers and guarded rollback. Initial network failures were a stale guest projection reference in the new fixture; the corrected fixture resolves the current owner display on each wait. Tests use disposable storage, not real saves. The original 28-card oracle/effect tests remain; only their exact catalog-size assumption was widened for intentional additions. Changed exact wiring scopes were retired only where their literal was replaced; the owning function/file remainder stays conservatively covered. Broader release evidence and separate-PC/internet play are not certified here.

## Second affinity card expansion (CE03)

CE03 builds on CE02 `3acce7b9b0342f568b335b030a775bcf739dc983`, on the existing `combat/ce02-affinity-cards` branch. Main and production persistence remain unchanged. Current implemented families and checkpoint tuning follow; all live values remain authoritative in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`, searchable by displayed name.

### Water checkpoint

Water adds **16 cards**, catalog **78** at its checkpoint (101 after CE03). `water_combat.gd` owns one shared FIFO of captured pressure amounts, with independently expiring Drenched reductions. Pressure has no decay: valid credits remain while Drenched is maintained, so a slow ordinary Form-only build still reaches Surge. Invalid sources are removed; idle Drenched expiry ends the meter. `water_tidal_return.gd` plans each consumed 100 points into per-player damage/healing shares; largest-remainder rounding preserves the single event total. Native full-share potency is **6 damage / 6 healing**, independently upgradeable. Each source emits a 450 ms outgoing wave from its captured position, then a 450 ms return with 70% of the width. Baseline half-width **0.70 m**, native outward push **0.20 m**. Solid cover/boundaries stop it; water/low cover use TC01 projectile clearance. Native enemy movement/resistance validates push. Each wave keeps separate per-spawn damage and per-recipient healing receipts. Returning restoration uses the existing attributed healing/feedback path and ordinary Slow cleanse; no extra Catalyst or Saturation. Dewfall restores **2/3 ordinary/refined HP** on its existing once-per-recipient contacts, with Dew Shelter still granting only its own one Ward.

An accepted timeline record owns all contributors' waves for one threshold; it never produces four full-strength Surges. A stale/fallen/disconnected contributor's pending waves are cancelled, without transferring its share. Floor changes invalidate the event. A refused schedule preserves all pressure and the Refreshing Strikes preparation. Final return contact resolves before timeline retirement, including a frame crossing its deadline. Monsoon preserves the normal wave and adds **two** expanding/contracting rings, each **35%** of that owner's weighted damage/healing, **3 m** radius, **180 ms** stagger. No ring or secondary contact generates pressure or more Monsoons.

### Wood checkpoint

Wood adds **11 cards**, catalog **89** at its checkpoint (101 after CE03). The existing Wood handler owns native Bark grants and splinter responses. Capacity still absorbs 40% of unblocked hits and expires per grant, with no pool ceiling. New volleys extend the native fan/link fragment resolution, not a second projectile queue. Penetration follows the actual lane and terrain; one enemy can be damaged once per volley. Core activation and captured absorption, Form fragmentation/primary specialization, Catalyst offense/capacity and held-Bark pressure remain separate effects. Captured retaliation belongs to a valid source and uses one source/recipient interval; independently learned Splinterguard and Splinterback add their authored fragments.

Splinterburst uses the existing acquired-action RPC, identity and cooldown state alongside Bloodletting. It consumes actual unexpired remaining Bark once, deals **1 damage per Bark** in **3 m**, and has a **6 s** cooldown. Expenditure bypasses absorption, break and retaliation hooks. Refused/stale/duplicate requests spend no extra protection. Values are provisional, without a maximum burst damage ceiling.

### Vital checkpoint

Vital adds **12 cards**, final CE03 catalog **101**. Native strongest-active-source capacity/regeneration resolution remains. New source investments are compiled into personal Vital potency across functioning sources, so another stronger grant cannot erase the investment; old Greening Chime remains Catalyst-specific. No unconditional additive grant stacking and no free healing on capacity gain. Growth expiration clamps through PlayerVitals. `vital_combat.gd` resolves native grant lifetimes, offensive preparations, Max-HP-based primary hits and Harvest resource spending; numerical definitions remain in the catalog.

Flourishing Assault grants **0.50 / 0.75 / 1.00 / 1.25%** of effective Max HP as primary basic-hit damage per copy, rounded once per attack, without spending health or requiring Harvest. Harvest uses the shared acquired-action interface as a toggle: an accepted ordinary launch spends up to **2 filled bonus HP**, yielding **2 damage per HP actually spent**, once per attack and on its first eligible direct contact. Normal HP and maximum capacity remain unchanged. A miss spends the launch resource; a refused attack does not. Empty reserves suspend the bonus without disabling the toggle. Recipient life/floor/expedition changes invalidate the toggle; no damage-taken, protection or affinity proc is issued by spending. Deep Roots is unimplemented.
