### Final CE03 verification and editing

Final source evidence: **30 focused suites / 51 process verdicts** pass at current input keys, with stable inputs and intact original logs: `.godot/validation/final-four-ce03-complete-20261120-213306/summary.json`. This includes all six expanded families, CE01 fail-closed requirements, the original card consumers, Reaper/Oracle, graft/rollback controls, actual attack queues, terrain, pure UI, three independent two-player pairs and three four-player family episodes, the retained shared-affinity four-player loop, fifth-client refusals, and matching/mismatched build admission. The mismatch pair admits zero guests; the matching guest reaches authoritative action readiness. Native paced solo Water, weighted Monsoon ring rounding, source fall/disconnect/life/floor invalidation, real Petal Fan spending and all three active transformations on one locked build have focused regressions. The slow-Form regression exposed and removed unintended per-credit pressure expiry before final acceptance. Earlier failed attempts remain retained.

The 62 CE02 definitions (including all original 28), rarity contributions and `PLAYTEST` settings compare identical. The catalog is **93 numerical / eight Unique**. Acquisition limit 12, numerical copy limit four, graft purchase limit six and reroll prices 2/4/6 are unchanged. New source investments that intentionally affect several Vital slots display those scopes. `card_active_actions.gd` remains an action adapter, never another acquisition ledger. Bloodletting, Splinterburst and Harvest use the same identity/replay checks and independent per-action state. Settings exposes four configurable additional bindings; the first two default to Z/X, later slots are unbound until assigned. B/Q/R remain unchanged.

Tune a named card in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`; tune native Water pressure/healing in `water_combat.gd`, tide timing/geometry in `water_tidal_return.gd`, native Bark/splinters in `wood_affinity.gd`, and growth/regeneration in `vital_combat.gd`. Tide policy/tuning enters Water's version data and the existing definition fingerprint. The retired radial Surge function and radius fields are removed. Generated effects use existing damage, health, status and timeline owners. Shared test observation counts admitted tide records rather than multiplying contributor visuals.

Validation-tool self-tests: **106 passed**, `.godot/validation/final-four-step1-tools-20261120-214004/summary.json`. The final audit verifies all 30 current keys and original log hashes. No runtime or fixture inputs changed after that acceptance run.

Registry: **252 to 261** suites. Shared definition changes conservatively invalidate the broader 261-suite source plan; only these 30 affected checks are refreshed, not a release-evidence campaign. Tests use verified disposable storage. Production persistence remains disabled; no real saves, main, protected tags, retained design references, neural decisions, RPC signatures or protocol-41 compact layout are changed. Manual balance, rendered desktop acceptance, separate-PC/internet multiplayer and packaging remain unverified. Conductor, Mirrorstep, Singularity and Deep Roots are not implemented by CE03.

## Modern gameplay cutover

| Retired execution | Authoritative replacement / retained data |
|---|---|
| Priced checkpoint ranks, legacy 166-card catalog, power/Technique counters, old cached offers and card event consumers | `affinity_card_catalog.gd` preserves the original 28 definitions and now includes the CE02 additions; `affinity_card_ledger.gd` owns offers and per-copy acquisitions. Game coordinates free choices and atomic Essence rerolls. |
| Installed fourth graft slot, replacement/success rolls, old graft panel and Rift Hook run combination | `expedition_grafts.gd` owns four reinforcements and six current adaptations; `native_grafts.gd` executes their scoped effects. All 24 monster material IDs and permanent Garden research/history remain. No additional adaptations are implied. |
| Species-selected Core skills, old Core cards/variants, generic Core Mark, Thorn Line adaptation dispatch | `plant_affinities.gd`, `affinity_build.gd` and nine family handlers resolve all 24 species / 48 materials. `core_skill_rules.gd` retains weapon delivery definitions; `core_combat.gd` executes the family contract. Reusable visuals remain separate. |
| Historical mask skill replacements, generic all-slot stat/tag effects, obsolete class action modes | Five connected-tree modules and their class handlers are the only executable class progression. Historical node order/IDs/prerequisites remain read-only conversion data in `class_catalog.gd`, `warden_tree.gd` and `subclass_catalog.gd`; their old effects are not applied. |
| Combat-mode launch switch and actor mode flag | `combat_ruleset.gd` always publishes the modern version plus definition signature. Terrain/save-root isolation remains independent. Old launcher arguments cannot select historical gameplay. |

Historical invested profiles remain readable without silently changing their selections. The approved explicit **Refund historical class choices** action at the Heart calls `Character.refund_historical_class()` inside the existing authorized profile transaction. It archives that class's original bits in `quarantine.learned_class_masks`, clears only its active mask/path, and records a version-1 `connected_class_migrations` marker. Earned Class XP is retained using `class_experience.gd`'s existing idempotent whole/fractional conversion. The current thresholds determine available connected selections; no extra points, new XP curve, or automatic focus is granted. Unconverted invested classes must refund before departing. Other classes' investments remain untouched. Invalid/unknown conversion versions require review.

The old actor fields `run_upgrades`, `checkpoint_offers`, `offer_floor`, `last_offer`, `power`, `technique`, `graft_part`, `graft_floor`, `graft_choice`, and `rift_hook` no longer own runtime state. Protocol-41 reliable projection keys reserved for retired fields remain inert empty/zero values; the fast snapshot layout is unchanged. The modern gameplay signature deliberately refuses older gameplay builds.

The modern HP scale is unconditional. The old mutable `CombatBalancePreview.enabled` gameplay switch and its old-heart branches are retired; `--combat-balance-preview` remains a **save-root isolation** argument only. Explicit-scale math in `combat_vitals.gd` still supports bounded data/unit conversion. Current damage, mitigation, healing, attack timing and enemy composition retain their former modern values. Seed/material descriptions now use explicit affinity identities rather than claiming old raw-tag passive bonuses.

Known modern enemy-learning gaps remain deliberately unexpanded: `AREA_PRESSURE` had a species-selected Ember Wake producer, but modern Fire has no authored production observation hook; shelter-break observations likewise came from the retired Core displacement path. Five volley/snare/artillery `CAST_DENIAL` responses (enemy kinds 1, 5, 6, 14 and 18) also have no current baseline interruption producer. Native interruption deliberately refuses those committed attacks. Their remaining fixtures distinguish supported native interruptions from recorded-observation consumer checks; they do not prove a live producer for the unsupported cases. Adding those producers requires an explicit AI/gameplay decision, rather than broadening native stagger or restoring Hush.

Inventory presentation also uses the current resolver: `Game.inventory_ui_data()` attaches a detached local view of the already-received `weapon_stats.affinity_build`; `inventory_panel.gd` calls `AffinityBuild.core_skill()` and `slot_preview()` for the Core label and all three slot descriptions. There is no `core_build` shim or species-description caller. The wire inventory and protocol-41 fast state are unchanged.

Historical **persistence** names such as `SaveBatch.Legacy`, `take_legacy_notices`, and contained serializer routes are not selectable old combat. They still protect live save transactions and issued guest-profile digests. Production AC-06 remains disabled. Uncommitted permanent rewards are held in host memory; a host process failure can lose work not yet settled. This cutover adds no durable journal and does not claim production persistence closure.


## Cutover measurements

Compared with the preserved AR14 parent (tracked runtime GDScript, excluding tests and tools):

| Measure | Before | After |
|---|---:|---:|
| Game lines / functions | 4,820 / 268 | 4,479 / 257 |
| Game plain top-level variables | 153 | 153 |
| Runtime GDScript lines | 56,057 | 51,753 |
| Actor combat-mode gates | 42 | 0 |
| Mutable health-mode reads | 78 | 0 |
| Retired runtime scripts | — | 10 |
| Retired gameplay-only fixtures | — | 26 |

Eleven Game purchase/install/support functions and ten mutable legacy actor fields are removed, rather than forwarded through a new service. No new state owner or parallel authority was added. Historical profile tables and inert wire keys are explicitly distinguished from executable gameplay; ordinary current owner operations remain. The card definitions and their effect consumer file are byte-for-byte unchanged from the parent; the five connected-tree definitions and family tuning remain unchanged.

## Cutover verification

Final focused verification passed 73 distinct fixtures: 58 units, one title-based solo smoke, 12 independent host/guest pairs, and two four-player controls including fifth-client refusal (93 fixture process exits). Current fixture hashes and strict per-peer verdicts were checked; original failed runs remain preserved. Coverage includes the five connected trees, all nine affinity families, all 28 real card consumers, current cumulative grafts, explicit historical refunds and XP conversion, TC01, inventory interaction, rewards, guarded rollback, floor travel, extraction, reconnect and guest-profile recovery. The card and four-player controls exercise actual integrated combat and owner projections. Recorded enemy-learning consumer checks remain distinct from the missing producers noted above.

The final inventory test opens the real panel, refreshes it, and inspects Core/Form/Catalyst equipment details. Modernized enemy fixtures respect current attack deadlines, participation reset refusal, and actual native Root Quake damage/displacement. These fixture corrections do not change combat tuning.

At cutover the planner had 214 suites. The later duplicate-mismatch retirement leaves 213 registered suites and preserves the definition-mismatch, matching-build and separate RPC-contract controls. The current structural audit has zero scope/unmapped-input warnings. The native-copy planner unit passed. This is local loopback, not separate-PC or internet verification; no release export or production save activation was performed. The existing approved playtest and six local design references are preserved.

## Current restructuring plan

### Approved remaining targets — 2026-11-19

The user has approved the following four remaining refactoring targets, in this order. After these are complete and verified, resume plants, grafting and upgrade-card development. This is the stopping boundary for the current restructuring effort; do not automatically extend it into more architectural milestones.

| Order | Target | Bounded completion criteria | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Garden commands and transactions | Complete the existing observation, conduit and return/discovery command boundaries in coherent slices. Domain rules accept focused facts and named intentions; existing owners retain research, crops, inventory and reward authority. Preserve guarded outcome/publication order and remove the replaced Game implementations. | Complete; focused verification below |
| 2 | Specialist combat and status effects | Narrow the current Core, affinity, class and graft execution interfaces around explicit sources, targets, statuses and effect lifetime. Preserve current values, proc lineage, host authority and existing owners; retire replaced broad execution paths. | Complete; focused verification below |
| 3 | Actor, session and travel integration | Replace unrestricted actor/world access at the remaining SessionWorld and ExpeditionTransitions boundaries with explicit actor facts and owner operations. Preserve admission, participation, settlement and lifecycle identities without creating competing records or another general player manager. | Complete; focused verification below |
| 4 | Editable UI scenes | Move suitable static layouts from runtime builders into editable Godot scenes. Keep controllers, rooted styling, detached view models and domain commands separate; preserve visual behavior and existing control identities. | Complete; focused verification below |

Human readability and a single authoritative definition remain primary acceptance criteria. Each target must simplify a real editing path, remove superseded ownership/execution and show focused behavior equivalence. Retain legitimate world composition and transaction coordination in Game; line-count reduction alone is not completion. Measure representative performance where execution changes.

Validation ownership and documentation are maintained throughout these four targets, not a fifth independent refactoring milestone. Improve test boundaries alongside each actual interface change, preserve live coverage and original failed evidence, and keep the current editing paths in this document accurate. Production AC-06 deployment gates, host-crash recovery and mid-run reconnection remain separate from this approved scope.

### Garden command boundary — target 1

`garden_commands_world.gd` is the current peer-ID command/scene/transaction integration. It handles observation, conduit and Garden research/care/plant requests; it owns no second progression store. `garden_interaction_service.gd` now contains pure request/admission/nearest-point rules. `garden_relationships.gd` accepts only bed/recipe facts. `garden_conduit.gd::choose` returns an uncommitted successor; Progress still owns its saved configuration. `garden_return_service.gd` operates on Progress and an immutable `Member` value containing character/run/participation facts. Return receipts can finish after the network peer and actor have been released; public return inspection is detached. Existing `garden_interaction_state.gd` and `garden_return_state.gd` remain the saved-record policies.

Game is **3373 → 3320 lines**, **241 → 241 functions**, **93 → 94 variables**. Four domain modules lose **78 literal Game/world member sites → 0**. Their integration is explicit in the 301-line port, which retains **144 world-member sites**; these are relocated integration, not eliminated whole-project coupling. No actor, profile, wallet, crop or receipt authority is duplicated. Research costs, plant/card/graft values, saved schemas, 25 RPC declarations and protocol-41 layout are unchanged.

Verified **17 suites / 32 strict process verdicts**, including seven independent host/guest pairs and two four-player controls with fifth-client refusal; **106 tooling self-tests** and **29 changed/caller scripts** compile. Coverage includes malformed/stale/duplicate requests, detached state, real guest inspection/rejoin, conduit crop-policy snapshots, frozen disconnected-character return receipts, guarded rollback/uncertainty, terrain, matching readiness and explicit mismatch refusal. Three pre-existing fixture setups were corrected for explicit owned beds, researched observations and one cinematic per shell. The private-inventory comparison excludes only its changing feedback notice; all inventory facts remain equal. Initial failed/incomplete runs are retained, including a runner without UDP permission; accepted network verdicts use permitted local loopback.

Median is **1.853 ms** for 300 synchronous world/six-Garden-view updates after 40 warmup, four players, three beds and Root Irrigation enabled. This headless CPU workload excludes physics, sockets and GPU work; it is not FPS. Source ownership remains conservative: **238 suites registered**, and shared wiring invalidates broad evidence; only the affected checks were refreshed. Production AC-06 remains disabled and real saves are untouched. Remaining work follows targets 2–4 above, then plants/grafts/cards.

### Specialist effect timeline — target 2

| Editing location | Authoritative boundary |
|---|---|
| `combat_effect_timeline.gd` | Owns private native records, active Core casts and record identity. `schedule_native`, `update_native`, `remove_native`, detached inspections, `advance_native`, Core admission/commit and savepoints are the only storage/execution path. The native cap remains 24; the graft subset cap remains 16 in `native_grafts.gd`. Delayed callbacks cannot execute newly scheduled effects recursively in the same frame or restore an obsolete Core cast. |
| `native_effects_world.gd` | A weak world integration performs source life/floor/connection checks, pulses, hit resolution, route work and ending visuals. Existing OutgoingHits, vitals, Ward and specialist class/graft/affinity handlers retain their mechanics. The owner itself never receives Game or actors. |
| Existing handlers and visuals | `core_combat.gd` advances detached cast frames and explicitly commits them; `combat_traits.gd` creates fields/traps/hits and retains their native rules. Graft and class annotations explicitly update accepted records. **`combat_effects.gd` remains the unchanged visual renderer**, separate from the timeline. |

Game's writable `trait_events` and hidden `core_combat` metadata are retired with no fallback alias. Guarded/contained rollback snapshots now restore the timeline explicitly, including a real aborted-transaction fixture that schedules tentative native/Core effects. Game changes **3320 → 3319 lines**, **241 → 241 functions**, **94 → 95 variables**. This is ownership/execution improvement, not substantial Game line deletion: `combat_traits.gd` shrinks 220 → 165 lines; the timeline has 88 lines and zero Game-member sites, and its execution port has 90 lines / 24 world-member sites. Specialist world adapters and the Game-owned leader status dictionary remain integration dependencies; no claim is made that every affinity helper is pure.

Verified **28 suites / 42 strict process verdicts**, covering all five trees, nine Core families, 28 card consumers, native grafts, traps, fields, source expiry, separate direct/secondary attribution, TC01, animation, rollback, six independent host/guest pairs and two four-player controls with overflow refusal. Current scripts compile (38 changed/caller scripts). Earlier alias-dependent fixture reads now re-inspect detached records after advancing; manual route changes explicitly commit. Original failed attempts remain recorded. The visual-renderer filename collision was corrected before gameplay acceptance and its original source/scopes are restored.

Synthetic owner overhead is **95 μs median** over 1,000 advances after 40 warmup, 24 records and four source IDs. It measures copies, validity callbacks, pulse scheduling and commits with a synthetic executor; it excludes real targeting/damage, full-frame cost, physics, network and rendering. No FPS or speedup claim. Registry **238 → 239**; replaced Core/native execution overlays retire while current owner/file coverage remains conservative. Broad release evidence is not refreshed. Protocol 41, RPCs/NodePaths, content values and save schemas remain unchanged. Production AC-06 is disabled; disposable saves and local loopback only. Target 3 follows next; then target 4 and the agreed return to content work.

### Actor, session and travel integration — target 3

| Editing location | Interface and authority |
|---|---|
| `network_session.gd::admission_error` | One owner policy accepts explicit protocol/content/identity/settlement/expedition facts, then queries its own accepted bindings. It owns no copied settlement or participation records. The refusal order/messages and four-player limit are unchanged. |
| `session_world.gd` | Game composes one weak world port. Host/join/registration/rejection/recovery/request-budget operations receive scalar arguments and profile payloads, never another Game or mutable actor record. Godot RPCs remain on Game. |
| `expedition_transitions.gd` | The composed weak port exposes peer-ID entry, admission, return, abandonment and checkpoint operations. Private actor lookup requires the current session link. Deferred entry/abandonment and collective travel retain captured connection bindings; a reused peer cannot inherit an old intent. Participation remains the membership/run/floor owner. |
| `loadout_materials.gd::departure_plan` | Pure selected-slot/cargo/available/personal facts produce detached demand/reservation arrays or the existing refusal. No material is consumed. PartyTravel retains collective reservation/consent coordination and uses explicit transition operations. |

The two ports remove **32 public Game parameters → 0** and remove mutable actor parameters from the four public entry/admission/return/abandon operations. **86 runtime/fixture consumers** use the composed operations; obsolete static/preload dispatch and fixture aliases are retired. No participant, character, reward or transport record is duplicated. Game changes **3319 → 3320 lines**, **241 → 241 functions**, **95 → 97 variables**. The two weak integration handles explain the variable increase. This is an interface/policy improvement, not a claim that world integration disappeared: literal member sites in the two ports are **628 → 630**, while admission and departure planning accept focused facts. Game still owns the actor registry, and these ports privately coordinate it with saves, presentation and the existing owners.

Verified **21 suites / 32 strict process verdicts** with current keys/log hashes and disposable save roots. Coverage includes explicit admission/refusal, request identity, stale peer reuse, loadout costs, party votes, exactly-once rewards, full-run forfeiture, revival, cards/grafts, guarded rollback and uncertainty, seven independent host/guest pairs, four-player mixed-class control and fifth-client refusal. 89 changed/caller scripts compile; 239 suites remain registered and source-scope warnings are zero. An original baseline composite reward assertion failed once before edits; two subsequent focused runs preserve and pass the same assertion. Its failure message now includes component facts, and the original failed evidence remains available; no reward values were changed to make it pass.

Protocol 41, snapshot/RPC contracts, card/graft/class/affinity values and save schemas are unchanged. Local loopback is not internet/separate-PC verification. Broad release evidence remains conservatively stale after shared wiring changes. Production AC-06 stays disabled; no real saves are used. Target 4 completes this approved refactoring sequence, then content work resumes.

### Editable gameplay shell — target 4 and stopping boundary

`ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.tscn` now authors **96 static nodes**: the menu, field guide, appearance choices, inventory/bestiary mounts, prompts, travel/connection frames, courier/recollection/checkpoint frames and confirmations. Open the scene in Godot to change their hierarchy, text, anchors, spacing and minimum sizes. Named `%Control` bindings keep the controller readable. Its old `_ready` construction and two support/checkpoint builders are retired, with no fallback builder. Dynamic card copies, inventory/bestiary rows, feature-owned content and custom HUD drawing remain dynamic.

`ui/gameplay/rooted_frame.gd` exposes scene padding and uses the unchanged `root_ui.gd::root_style` artwork/margin policy; it contains no gameplay. `gameplay_ui.gd` applies the shared theme before children initialize, connects intentions and presents detached data. It changes **621 → 426 lines**, **49 → 49 functions**. The scene expands 15 → 616 text lines; this is an explicit editable layout, not a claim of net text deletion. Styling stays authoritative in `root_ui.gd`; gameplay/card values stay in their catalogs.

Final verification passes **22 suites / 31 strict process verdicts**, **106 validation-tool self-tests**, changed-script compilation, a completed headless editor scene load, and GPU-rendered session/card/HUD views inspected visually. A before/after standalone comparison matches **28 named controls across 11 states**, including visible geometry, text, focus/mouse policies and rooted margins. Source tests cover authored nodes before controller execution, exclusive focus, card revisions/rerolls, actual card effects, class selection, inventory, progression, courier, travel, guarded settlement/forfeiture, five independent host/guest pairs, four-player mixed-class play, fifth-player refusal and Garden water filling.

The conversion scripts briefly damaged UTF-8 punctuation; the original strings are restored, including six strings changed during target 3, with no gameplay calculation change. Original failed layout attempts remain recorded. A restricted tooling run could not create its disposable hard-link fixture; the permitted rerun passes all 106 tests. Short editor attempts ended before file scanning completed and were not counted as acceptance; the final bounded load reaches **Editor layout ready** without errors. Fourteen unrelated UID sidecars regenerated by the editor are removed; only the new frame's sidecar is added.

**All four approved targets are complete.** Game changes **3373 → 3320 lines**, **241 → 241 functions**, **93 → 97 variables** across the sequence. Real ownership gains are the effect timeline and pure domain decisions; private world ports still integrate Game's actor records and saves. There is no claim that all world coupling is eliminated. Registry 238 → 239 adds one timeline fixture. Shared wiring/map changes conservatively invalidate broad release evidence; only focused evidence is refreshed. Production AC-06, host-crash recovery and separate-PC/internet multiplayer verification remain separate. No export or real-save write is performed. Return to **plants, grafting and upgrade cards**; do not automatically start another architecture milestone.

### Earlier plan and completed work

The 2026-11-18 source/research review changes the priority from additional small field extractions to complete behavior boundaries. Game is the world-scene composition and integration root: it wires dependencies, routes authorized requests, coordinates start/transition/exit and advances ordered simulation/publication stages. UI construction, class execution, outgoing-hit rules and snapshot assembly should have explicit owners. Line count is a symptom; editing clarity, actual dependency reduction and measured frame work are the acceptance criteria.

| Step | Boundary and acceptance | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1a | Gameplay UI shell, HUD presentation, overlay lifecycle, checkpoint cards, travel and confirmation controls. One scene/controller owns controls and presentation state. It accepts focused detached display data and emits explicit local requests; it never receives Game or mutable actors. Remove replaced Game fields/builders and invisible superseded HUD work. Keep RPC entry points and domain transactions in their current owners. | Implemented and verified as the bounded first slice below. |
| 1b | Finish world-facing feature-panel ports. Replace the remaining broad feature-panel Game dependencies with focused read/command contracts as complete features; do not disguise them inside the shell owner. Move appropriate static layouts into editable scenes as those boundaries stabilize. | Inventory, gate-loadout, Tab, progression/research/story, Garden Altar, expedition graft and planting feature ports are complete. The growth cinematic port is also complete. Observation, crop/care, station, return, conduit and ambience views are now detached below. Existing domain services remain Game-bound; suitable editable static scenes remain pending. |
| 2 | Player combat execution: coherent native class-action admission/execution and outgoing-hit resolution using existing class/affinity/graft handlers. Establish shared resolved ability/build facts for combat, previews and descriptions, with explicit class/loadout/card/graft invalidation. | Shared Q/R execution, outgoing contacts/native hit batches, ordinary weapon launch/projectile lifecycle, and shared resolved combat values/tooltips are complete below. Casts and aim previews share range math. Specialist source/status ports remain coupled. Host publication and guest receive/reconciliation are complete below; remaining world presentation is separate. |
| 3 | Replication and remaining world integration: snapshot/detail builders consume owner projections; transport/RPC NodePaths, protocol 41, cadence/freshness and payload budget remain protected. Garden/session commands retain domain authorization and atomic settlement. | Host publication cadence, sequence/recovery ownership and detached public/owner projections are complete below. Guest receive/reconciliation ownership is complete below. Host reliable-health publication, base world guidance and the ordered effects/audio/map/Garden presentation pipeline are complete below. Avatar, spectator and dropped-item views now use detached facts below. Cultivation rules and plant/care/harvest integration are now separated below. Observation, conduit and return/discovery adapters remain coupled. Production AC-06 deployment is separate. |

For performance, first remove confirmed redundant work and then measure the same representative workload before/after. Do not assume moving code or adding more Nodes makes execution faster. UI-only changes should gain narrower test/change impact where the interface really permits it, without weakening shared-contract coverage. Preserve current implementations until their replacement works, then retire the replaced paths instead of keeping a selectable fallback.

The reference guidance is Godot's [scene organization](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/best_practices/scene_organization.html), [autoloads versus regular nodes](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/best_practices/autoloads_versus_regular_nodes.html), [lightweight objects](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/best_practices/node_alternatives.html) and [CPU measurement](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/performance/cpu_optimization.html). These are design principles, not a prescribed maximum size for game.gd or a reason to replace readable text content definitions.

## Gameplay UI ownership

The first plan slice starts at `c0f3db8c1186d8caffc1379a875ada00c9d102d6`, recoverable through `architecture/pre-gameplay-ui`. [`ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.tscn`](../ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.tscn) is a child of the existing world root; its controller owns HUD/menu controls, overlay visibility, confirmations and the displayed checkpoint revision. It receives detached presentation values and emits `requested(command, data)` and `overlay_opened`. It never receives Game, a mutable actor, a progression owner or a wallet. `inventory_drop_surface.gd` likewise has only its inventory frame and an item-drop intent signal. No second offer, acquisition, participant or balance authority is introduced.

| Responsibility / editing location | Interface and integration |
|---|---|
| Shell, HUD, prompts and menu frames: [`ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.gd`](../ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.gd) | `present_hud`, `present_context`, `present_feedback`, `present_menu` and `present_connection` receive display facts. `toggle_panel`, `panel_is_open`, `close_panels` and `toggle_external` govern one exclusive overlay lifecycle. `overlay_opened` lets Game cancel held aiming. The scene establishes the CanvasLayer/Surface; the retained rooted layouts are constructed in the controller. Moving suitable static layouts into editable scenes remains part of 1b. |
| Checkpoint card face and Details: `GameplayUI.show_checkpoint_offer` | Renders the detached ledger projection. Take buttons capture their own offer revision; Skip and Reroll address the displayed revision. Reopening does not generate an offer. `Game.open_checkpoint_cards` retains floor admission; existing card/wallet transactions still validate, acquire and spend. Card balance stays solely in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`. |
| Travel display, courier and confirmations: `GameplayUI.present_travel`, `show_deposit`, `show_abandon_confirmation`, `show_title_confirmation` | Receives vote/item display data and emits ready/bank/confirmed-exit requests. It neither edits participant votes nor authorizes departures. Existing whole-run forfeiture, protected courier deliveries and guarded outcomes are unchanged. |
| Authorized integration: `Game.configure_gameplay_ui`, `_handle_ui_request`, `request_host_session` | Wires presentation signals to existing domain commands. Title entry calls the host operation directly instead of simulating a click on Game's button. All 25 Game RPC annotations/signatures and the world root NodePath stay unchanged. App-level settings/network dialogs remain integration-owned. |
| Feature content: `mount_feature`, `show_loadout`, `show_progression`, `show_graft`, `show_altar`, `show_planting`, `toggle_summary` | The shell owns frames and visibility. All seven mounted features receive detached facts and emit requests; none receives Game. Garden world/cinematic adapters outside these features remain separate work. |
| Shared rooted styling and existing HUD drawing: [`root_ui.gd`](../root_ui.gd) | Pure `panel`, `label`, `button` factories replace Game utility calls. This script remains the visible combat HUD renderer; it is not an actor-state owner. Root border art, layout dimensions and gameplay values remain unchanged. |

The four obsolete hidden readouts (`title`, `health_label`, `skill_label`, `inventory`) and their string-building work are retired; their parent panel was permanently hidden. The illustrated inventory, party HUD, class/Core indicators and Tab stats remain active. Game keeps domain feedback/transaction notice state; the shell owns its visual projection, not another feedback authority.

| Consistent source measurement | Before | After |
|---|---:|---:|
| `game.gd` physical lines | 4,478 | 4,029 |
| `game.gd` functions | 256 | 247 |
| Plain top-level `var` declarations | 154 | 99 |
| Selected Game UI/presentation fields | 55 | 0 |
| External direct accesses to those Game UI fields | 32 | 0 |
| External Game UI-factory/overlay calls | 48 | 0 |
| All external `game.member` mentions (methods and data combined) | 3,744 | 3,719 |
| External `game.players` mentions | 343 | 344 |
| Runtime GDScript lines (excluding tests/tools) | 52,489 | 52,627 |

The 55 selected fields are removed from Game: 51 become view-owned controls/registry state and four hidden readouts disappear. Twelve Game builders/helpers retire; three composition/request functions replace their integration responsibilities. One `@onready` UI handle is added (the plain-`var` count excludes it). The controller and drop surface have zero Game/actor inputs and no mutable Game data access. Generic `game.member` totals are reported separately because replacing a field access with an owner operation is **not** elimination of all coupling. Total runtime lines grow with the explicit UI contract: this is ownership and editing-path improvement, not a claim of net project-code deletion.

One host Wilds render frame now reuses one scout snapshot instead of building two and resolves native Q/R definitions twice rather than three times. Those are source-confirmed redundant operations removed; no FPS improvement or full-frame benchmark is claimed. This earlier UI slice left combat/status calculations, world prompts and resolved build/tooltip caching separate. The shared combat-value boundary below now completes the last of those items.

The first slice left feature ports pending. The later cutovers below complete all seven mounted panels, growth playback and the six remaining Garden views. Their controls receive detached data and emit requests. `garden_presentation_world.gd` is the single Garden display integration port; existing Garden command services still receive Game. Avatar/spectator/loot rendering is now detached below. Specialist combat ports and suitable editable static scenes remain separate work. Game retains scoped fact capture, authorized commands and app-dialog integration; no whole-project UI-independence claim is made.

Focused verification on 2026-11-18 passed **23 current source suites / 33 fixture-process verdicts**: 16 unit fixtures, six independent two-player pairs and one four-player card/mixed-class control including fifth-client refusal. Six baseline suites / seven peer verdicts and 85 changed-script compile checks passed; 106 validation-tool self-tests passed. Current checks include all 28 real card consumers, visible HUD/Tab/menu controls, aimed-input cancellation, inventory/planting/Heart/crop prompts, host/guest watering, floor votes, extraction, ledger continuity and disposable-save transaction rollback. The matching build joined and passed authoritative action readiness; the bad-definition build received the exact ruleset refusal with zero guest admissions. Initial compile errors and obsolete prompt-fixture calls were corrected; their original failed verdicts are retained outside the repository with the final source-key evidence. All final keys match the live source and every fixture has a verified disposable save profile. Registry count is 219 → 220. The shared world/actor wiring and map changes invalidate all 220 source keys; only this bounded affected set was refreshed. Broad release/package evidence remains separately scheduled. No export, manual desktop acceptance, separate-PC/internet verification or production AC-06 activation is claimed.

## Player-build UI ports

This step starts at `e2eb05fb82b8ceef00eae51ae81c6794b722dab4`, protected by `architecture/pre-player-build-ui`. It completes one feature group: inventory, gate preparation and Tab stats. All three real panels run without Game, World, a mutable actor or a save owner. They own display/selection state, emit `refresh_requested` and `requested(command, data)`, and accept `present(detached_facts)`. No new authoritative equipment, profile, card, graft, wallet or transaction state is created.

| Editing location | Responsibility and narrow contract |
|---|---|
| [`inventory_panel.gd`](../inventory_panel.gd), [`inventory_slot.gd`](../inventory_slot.gd) | Inventory layout, filters, sort/pages, local ordering and selection. Input contains storage rows, equipment IDs, the resolved affinity build and a notice. Transfer emits source/target/item/quantity; it cannot edit inventory. Slot/Core descriptions still use `affinity_build.gd` and canonical item/weapon definitions. |
| [`loadout_panel.gd`](../loadout_panel.gd) | Gate layout, material browsing, native focus previews and the existing two-second unconfirmed-slot feedback. Input contains selected class/weapon, available counts, equipped/planned IDs, packed cargo, scoped class progression, native stats/card totals, gate/class eligibility and travel display. Selection/pack/class/focus requests use existing Garden commands. Departure is a request; Game checks current consent before choosing unready/depart. |
| [`run_summary_panel.gd`](../run_summary_panel.gd) | Tab layout, grouped card Details and focus. It receives summary facts, renders rows and requests close/refresh. It no longer reads an actor every frame or owns a World-taking projection. Its refresh remains every 200 ms; inventory/loadout retain their 250 ms cadence. |
| [`ui/gameplay/player_build_projection.gd`](../ui/gameplay/player_build_projection.gd) | Pure summary formatting and scoped class-profile extraction. Resolves the same actual acquired card contributions, damage bands, attacks/sec, native ability cooldowns, health/recovery and graft descriptions. Balance values remain in their existing class/card/graft definitions; this helper adds no gameplay tuning. It accepts detached facts, never Game or an actor. |
| `Game.inventory_ui_data`, `loadout_ui_data`, `run_summary_ui_data(peer_id)` | Integration reads existing authoritative owners and returns deep-detached results. The latter defaults to the local peer; explicit IDs support host-side inspection. Guest data stays subject to the existing reliable-details floor key. No query applies rewards or restores authoritative state. The class snapshot carries XP, upgrade masks, migration/quarantine data and the selected class path. |
| `GameplayUI.mount_feature`, `_feature_request`, `present_inventory`, `present_loadout`, `present_summary`, `summary_input` | Binds the three views before they enter the tree, routes refresh/action signals and supplies results. Game is not assigned to these views. The shell owns close/class-tree navigation; Game still validates commands and transactions. Summary input receives an eligibility Boolean, and existing HUD participation closes Tab on Garden return. |

Query flow is `panel refresh → shell request → Game reads owners → shell presents detached facts`. Mutation flow is `panel intention → existing authorized Game command → authoritative owner/transaction → next projection`. A rejected or pending request does not grant a focus, equip a part, consume inventory or advance travel. Guest tests deliberately mutate all three returned read models and confirm that replicated bags, available counts, canonical/actor profile XP and weapon details remain intact. Existing owned ledgers and immutable player components remain the only authorities.

| Measurement from this step's parent | Before | After |
|---|---:|---:|
| Game/World member mentions in the three panels | 116 | 0 |
| Panel Game fields | 3 | 0 |
| `game.gd` lines | 4,029 | 4,074 |
| `game.gd` functions | 247 | 249 |
| All external `game.member` mentions | 3,719 | 3,635 |
| External `game.players` mentions | 344 | 340 |
| Runtime GDScript lines | 52,627 | 52,703 |

The 116 panel mentions include data, commands and catalog/service aliases; they are not 116 separate mutable writes. Game grows by 45 lines/two functions to assemble explicit read projections at the current actor-registry boundary. That integration remains coupling, but it no longer leaks into the views or a new Game-taking adapter. Overall runtime grows by 76 lines for the interfaces and detached outputs. There is no duplicated authoritative cache, metadata store, universal UI service manager or FPS improvement claim. Existing polling cadence is preserved; summary actor reads now occur through the requested projection rather than each view frame. Non-Tab input short-circuits before actor eligibility lookups.

Retired runtime paths are the three `var game` fields, `RunSummary.handle_input(world, event)` and `RunSummary.project(world, player, stats)`. Thirty-eight layered source-normalization scopes for the fully replaced panels are retired; their current behavior stays covered through `player_build_ui` file ownership and integration suites. Surviving Game normalization baselines remain unchanged. No gameplay fixture is retired; one standalone boundary fixture is added.

Seven before-change controls / eight peer verdicts passed; 15 changed scripts compiled; 106 validation-tool self-tests passed. Coverage includes all five zero-XP focus choices, native Q/R availability, all 28 real card consumers, equipment previews/reservations, inventory transfer/pagination, mixed-rarity stat totals, actual damage/sustain/health bands, graft caps, aimed input, guarded rollback and guest departures. The guest read-model mutation probes preserve replicated bags, reservation counts, canonical/actor XP and weapon details. Matching-build authoritative action readiness and exact definition-mismatch refusal with zero authenticated guest admissions passed. Original failed verdicts are retained: the new guest isolation assertion initially assumed every guest actor held its character profile; it was corrected to verify both canonical and optional actor ownership. Initial compile errors were corrected before gameplay checks. The final input guard's six changed keys and aimed-input control were refreshed; every final key matches the committed candidate source. ## Progression, research and story presentation

This step begins at `5d17a052c58cdf593de5018199a804abfcdd3c03`, protected by `architecture/pre-progression-ui`. The complete [`progression_panel.gd`](../progression_panel.gd) and its [`garden_story_panel.gd`](../garden_story_panel.gd) child accept detached facts and emit intentions. Neither receives Game, World, a mutable actor, a profile owner or a save service. Seeds, Heart research, all five connected class trees and stories remain one discoverable UI feature; this is not a new progression authority.

| Interface | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `Progression.present(facts)` / `Stories.present(discovery_projection)` | Render known seeds, portraits, Heart research, connected paths and the latest twelve stories. The progression view copies its model; the story child retains rendered text/signature only. Catalog lookups remain at their canonical definitions. |
| `refresh_requested({tab, chosen})` | The view requests facts every 250 ms while visible and after local selection. Game gathers host owner facts or accepted guest caches in `progression_ui_data(selection)`, then the shell calls `present_progression`. Selected IDs are advisory input, not authorization. Story projection now runs only for its visible tab at this cadence, replacing its per-frame world lookup. |
| `requested(command, data)` | Learn/refund and research purchase use existing `garden_action` commands. Existing host/domain checks decide admission and spending. View callbacks cannot grant progression or debit materials. The root decides whether closing may return to live weapon-gate preparation; the view reports only its navigation intention. |
| `player_build_projection.gd::progression_profile(profile)` | Reuse the existing pure class-profile shaping rule and include all five connected paths for inspection. XP, masks and migration eligibility are detached; inventory, wallet and live actor records are absent. Switching into Classes adopts the active class. An explicitly inspected other class survives ordinary refreshes. |

Removed paths: two `var game` references; the story child's world-taking `_process`; the UI's duplicated offering-sort algorithm; permanently hidden seed-breeding/identify controls and their uncalled selection/dispatch methods. The supported Altar seed-combination and identification commands remain intact. No duplicate authoritative progression cache or optional old UI execution route remains. Deferred tree scrolling retains its existing version check.

## Garden Altar, graft and planting presentation

The local `architecture/pre-garden-feature-ui-20261118` tag preserves `c770472d040c54176a22efce3fa4f5027f6cef0f`. The three remaining mounted feature panels now use the same explicit display/request boundary as inventory and progression. Each feature owns only its controls, detached quote/inventory data and local selection or offering draft. None receives Game, an actor dictionary, a wallet or a persistence owner.

| Editing location | Display input and emitted operations |
|---|---|
| `affinity_grafting_panel.gd` | `set_data(quote)` copies the host ledger's detached Altar view. Confirm emits the existing revision/run/floor-bound graft request through `requested`. A pending request cannot be repeated. Graft definitions, eligibility, prices and acquisition ownership remain in `expedition_grafts.gd`. |
| `garden_altar_panel.gd` | `begin(snapshot)` / `set_data(snapshot)` copy display inventory, recipes, preview and revision. Dragging changes only the draft; preview, identify and combine emit `altar_action` with a detached draft and request revision. Older replies are ignored and replies during a drag are held. `altar_service.gd` remains the recipe/inventory transaction authority. |
| `planting_panel.gd` | `begin(bed, stock)` copies personal seed counts; `set_selection_enabled(allowed)` receives save-hold eligibility as a Boolean. The view reads authored seed/growth descriptions and emits the existing bed/species planting command. It does not inspect persistence or plant/debit anything locally. |
| `ui/gameplay/gameplay_ui.gd` | Wires each feature's requests, owns closing and accepts `present_world_access(kind, allowed)` / `present_planting_access(allowed)`. Game checks proximity only for the open graft/Altar panel. Save holds update the planting display through the existing feedback projection. Display data is copied at the feature boundary, without redundant shell copies. |
| `game.gd` | Composes the seven views and routes `altar_action` through the existing authorized request. Host transactions, RPC entry points, recipient authorization, ledger plans, wallet/inventory debits and reliable owner projections retain their existing authorities. |

Measured at this boundary: direct panel `game.member` mentions **16 → 0**; three unrestricted Game references and the graft view's per-frame world poll are retired. Game changes from **4,099 → 4,101 lines**, with **251 functions** and **101 top-level variables including `@onready`**, both unchanged. The additional integration lines are explicit authorization/read-model wiring, not a claim of smaller whole-project runtime or faster frames. No authoritative gameplay state is duplicated or moved into the views.

Focused test ownership: `garden_feature_ui` instantiates the real shell and all three panels without Game and checks detachment, revisioned requests, duplicate submission prevention, save holds and closing. `seed_inventory` now identifies through the real Altar button and plants through the real picker. `menu_coop` purchases through the guest's actual graft controls before verifying host damage and owner state. `cr05_native_grafts`, `guarded_gameplay_transactions` and `ec01_affinity_cards_party` retain domain rollback, guarded outcomes and four-player integration coverage. Eight obsolete exact-source overlays tied to the replaced panel implementations are retired; unrelated source baselines remain intact. No live gameplay fixture is removed.

Final focused verification passes seven suites with 12 structured unit/peer verdicts, including independent two-player and four-player loopback with fifth-client refusal. All 106 validation-tool self-tests pass. Exact source keys, evidence hashes and zero unmapped/mismatched-scope warnings were checked. ## Garden growth cinematic ownership

The local `architecture/pre-garden-cinematics-20261118` tag preserves `62f29610fcc8044dfbfde87c5d8282f2a5f8c5bd`. `garden_growth_view.gd` now owns private playback event/timer, viewer context, acknowledgement retry and captured interface visibility. It receives no Game, player dictionaries, progression owner or persistence session. Its event inspection getter returns a detached copy; `is_active()` derives the sole active flag from that event. `GameplayUI` holds a view reference and queries it; Game's duplicate `garden_cinematic_active` metadata is retired.

| Boundary | Operations and responsibility |
|---|---|
| `garden_growth_view.gd` | `configure(stage, points)` accepts presentation nodes only. `present(facts)` copies the small display contract: viewer context, pending events, position, safe/blocked/skip/start eligibility. `capture_interfaces(layers)` accepts only CanvasLayers. Existing root-tip camera, 8.8-second timeline, model restoration, Skip and one-second acknowledgement retry remain here. |
| `garden_presentation_world.gd::_cinematic_frame()` | The existing world adapter gathers the local viewer's queue and scalar eligibility. It answers refresh/interface requests and routes acknowledgement/audio intents to existing services. It holds only a weak world reference and retains Game integration; it is not another progression or receipt owner. |
| `GameplayUI.bind_cinematic()` / `cinematic_active()` | The shell exposes input eligibility through the renderer, including while its HUD is hidden. Game input/movement, aiming, developer tools and crop prompts query this operation instead of metadata. No second Boolean authority is stored. |
| `garden_growth_service.gd` | Still owns viewer registration, queue projection and host-authorized receipt acknowledgement through existing transactions. The renderer can request an acknowledgement, not commit it. Audio still goes through the existing receipt-deduplicated EventAudio route. |
| `garden_return_view.gd` | Uses `discovery_progress(id)` rather than inspecting the renderer's mutable event/timer. Stage-owned camera/formation metadata remains local presentation integration; this is not a Stage or terrain rewrite. |

Measured at this boundary: the renderer has **70 to zero literal Game-member mentions**; the existing world adapter has **28 to 44**. Many former references now address explicit Stage geometry or authored constants; these are relocated presentation dependencies, not 70 deleted gameplay dependencies. The affected files lose 54 literal Game-member mentions overall. Direct player/settings/session access is confined to the small integration read boundary. Game stays at **4,101 lines / 251 functions / 101 top-level variables including `@onready`**. No gameplay state owner or duplicate queue is added.

Steady active playback previously projected the queue in `_process` and twice in `animate`; it now requests one display update and reuses it for rendering. Begin/finish refresh separately when needed. This reduces redundant projections structurally; no FPS or frame-time improvement is claimed.

Current tests: `garden_cinematic_view` runs the real renderer/Stage without Game and checks detached inspection, camera/formation, interface restoration, viewer change, blocked playback and bounded retries. `garden_growth`, `heart_growth_visual`, `event_audio`, `garden_growth_coop`, `ability_aim`, `developer_garden`, guarded transaction and existing four-player fixtures cover integration. Nine superseded exact-source metadata/mount overlays are retired; unrelated scope baselines remain intact. Developer Settings also closes through `GameplayUI.close_panels()`; its one live call to the removed Game overlay wrapper is retired.

The baseline exposed stale Garden fixtures: automatic fourth-bed/station creation, the old native Heart counter on reload, and a guest wait for that counter. Tests now distinguish research permission, already-owned adopted beds and the current generated Heart. Queue, rollback, camera, no-reward, private acknowledgement and reconnect checks remain. The standalone renderer checks authored formation targets; it does not claim that generated construction uses those old formation tags. Actual new construction remains covered by the existing guarded `experimental_terrain` fixture. The failed original evidence is retained, rather than relabelled as passing. The Developer Garden fixture likewise checks bed permission without automatic construction, including rollback of the actual current three-bed state.

This checkpoint does not activate production AC-06 or change unguarded construction semantics. Generated construction expects typed guarded finalization, so an ad hoc unguarded world is unsuitable for a real placement test. The renderer fixture supplies an already-earned saved bed through the supported adoption path. Isolated guarded title/terrain tests retain actual placement coverage; production deployment and host-crash recovery remain separate work.

Final focused verification: 12 current suites pass with 17 strict unit/peer verdicts, including two-player private queues/reconnect and four-player combat with overflow refusal. All 106 validation-tool self-tests pass. After the Developer Settings correction, three changed-input suites were rerun; nine exact-input passes were retained with their original evidence hashes. The registry is 225 suites; no full release, package, manual visual or separate-PC/internet verification is claimed.

## Native class-action execution

The shared Q/R sequence previously in `Game.use_class_skill` now lives in [`native_class_actions.gd`](../native_class_actions.gd). `cast_world(world, peer, slot)` is the narrow integration entry; it constructs a [`ClassCaster`](../class_caster.gd) bound to one actor, life generation and expedition epoch. The actual `cast(caster, slot)` sequence receives no Game or actor dictionary. All six runtime entry calls, including aimed requests and the Neural Arena bot, use this path. The old Game method is removed, not retained as a fallback.

| Editing responsibility | Authoritative location / interface |
|---|---|
| Shared admission and execution order | `native_class_actions.gd::cast`: life/cleared/cooldown/Core admission, resolved native skill and Catalyst context, targeting refusal, native protection/traps/impacts, cooldown, one class completion, follow-ups and publication. A refused snare still spends no cooldown. The existing event-start ordering before target refusal is preserved. |
| Scoped world integration | `class_caster.gd`: `observe`, `resolved_stats`, `target_plan`, detached `ally_facts`, explicit Ward/guard operations, class-handler calls, cooldown commit and presentation. Only the adapter accesses its private actor/world. No public operation returns an actor, profile, service handle or mutable roster. Stale floor, life or replaced-actor captures are rejected before activation. |
| Tunable values | Keep values in `skill_catalog.gd`, `subclass_catalog.gd`, the five connected trees and their class handlers. `class_skill_balance.gd::range_limit` now supplies both actual casts and `class_ability_targeting.gd` previews; its formula and all timing/healing values are unchanged. Card values remain in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`; only one consumer-location annotation changes. |
| Existing specialized behavior | Focus Q execution stays in `subclass_combat.gd`; Warden, Weaver, Ranger, Reaper and Oracle native modifiers stay in their named combat modules. These handlers still have broad world/actor integration and are explicit remaining debt. Weaver captures the Catalyst target footprint before Symbiotic Nova extends native hits. Ward source/recipient claims and once-per-activation ordering stay with `combat_action_events.gd`. |
| Outgoing contacts and scheduling | `ClassCaster.apply_targets` now calls `outgoing_hits.gd::skill_world`; both former Game hit methods are retired by the next slice below. Projectile scheduling and specialized class/affinity effects remain with their existing implementations. No packet, RPC path, persistent schema, gameplay mode or extra state owner is introduced. |

Before/after at recovery tag `architecture/pre-native-class-actions-20261118`: Game **4,101 → 3,942 lines**, **251 → 250 functions**, **101 → 101 plain/onready variables**. Six runtime calls no longer pass mutable actors into shared class execution. The replacement is a 137-line sequence plus a 234-line integration adapter: total runtime code grows, while execution policy leaves Game. The adapter has 23 literal world-member references (including its two constructor reads); these are relocated integration dependencies, not eliminated dependencies. Its 40 focused operations avoid exposing the whole world to the execution sequence. No duplicate HP, cooldown, protection, class profile or cast queue is created. The only retained adapter records are the existing handlers' per-cast snare/Siphon/Chime results.

`native_class_actions` tests real zero-part native Q/R and rejects cooling, invalid-slot, missing-peer, stale-floor/life and replaced-actor calls before a new activation. CT01–CT05 retain actual node consumers, and the aiming, class/affinity contracts, cards, grafts, TC01, guarded transactions and independent pair/party controls cover integration. Thirty-three exact Game overlays and their old function ownership are retired; the suite registry grows **225 → 226**, with unrelated matching baselines unchanged. The Game signature/declaration change intentionally invalidates broad source evidence; focused results are not release certification. No full export is performed.

Final focused verification has **22 current-input suites / 31 strict per-process PASS verdicts**, including five independent host/guest class pairs and the four-player card control with fifth-client refusal, plus **106 validation-tool self-tests**. Seventeen matching results are retained without rerunning; five helper-dependent network fixtures are rerun after adding failure diagnostics. The health-preview fixture restores a complete authored floor before drawing the HUD, because its collision-only layout has no scenery/ExitGate data. All numerical assertions remain. One Oracle Ward/Portent observation failed intermittently in an earlier pair run; the subsequent instrumented pair passes. Failure-only witness data now records Ward/Mercy/guest timing and actual blocks; the intermittent cause is not claimed resolved. Original failing evidence is retained.

The reviewed connected plan invalidates **217 of 226** source suites; the remaining broad release-evidence refresh is deliberately deferred. All peer data roots are disposable `.godot/vd` profiles. Protocol 41, RPC signatures, snapshot bytes and save writers are unchanged. Correcting the card's consumer-location annotation changes the existing definitions fingerprint, so peers should run the same updated source/build; no transport schema changes. The native-action checkpoint adds bounded per-cast objects and at most four detached ally facts for a protection cast, not new per-frame processing. No frame-rate improvement is claimed. ## Outgoing enemy-hit resolution

[`outgoing_hits.gd`](../outgoing_hits.gd) owns the shared weapon/native/secondary contact calculation and native class-hit batch. The public world entries are `hit_world(world, source_peer, target, origin, stats, now, expected_token)`, `skill_world(world, source_peer, targets, skill, stats, now)` and the deliberately restricted `affinity_world(world, source_peer, target, hp, now, color)`. They construct a short-lived [`OutgoingHitContext`](../outgoing_hit_context.gd). The actual policy functions `hit(context, ...)` and `skill_targets(context, ...)` receive no Game or mutable actor dictionary. Both old Game methods are removed; there is no alternate damage engine.

| Responsibility / editing location | Contract and preserved behavior |
|---|---|
| Native damage and displacement policy | `outgoing_hits.gd::hit` retains graft/counter/class-charge augmentation, locked roll and explicit HP, Tracked Prey, shelter then Bark mitigation, exposure, pull/push, native control, damage and death/after-hit ordering. Existing movement constants are descriptively named at the top, with metres/milliseconds/scales identified. Enemy armor amounts remain in `enemy_balance.gd`; HP conversion remains in `combat_balance_preview.gd` / `combat_vitals.gd`. |
| Native class and trap hits | `skill_targets` retains initial root/recovery, per-target native bonuses, isolated-secondary classification, contact-based drain eligibility, one Echoing Growth response and the Heart threshold message. It captures each selected enemy's spawn identity before processing the batch. A synchronous death/observation hook cannot substitute a replacement enemy into a later contact. |
| Scoped integration and existing owners | `outgoing_hit_context.gd` binds the source actor reference, life, expedition epoch and encounter owner. It returns scalar facts, IDs and detached outcomes; it never returns an actor or service. `EncounterRoster.apply_damage` remains the sole HP write. Its additive `spawn_identity` query covers leaders and scouts without changing the existing scout-only `token()` convention. Tactical scout dictionaries and leader fields still have private bridge integration; this is not a completed tactical-state extraction. |
| Already-accepted affinity damage | `affinity_world` preserves the existing frozen secondary-HP continuation when its source is absent. The context admits that exception only for secondary Core-classified explicit HP, with no native charge/graft/weapon benefits or new player's threat credit. The former fallback actor lacked `frame`; the new bridge explicitly handles absence rather than pretending that a departed player is a full actor. Existing effect owners still decide expiry and source validity before delivery. |
| Delivery, obstructions and lineage | Weapon/class/Core consumers retain range, enemy contact budgets, TC01 projectile/sight checks and movement/ground-placement restrictions. The resolved-hit layer does not impose one blanket line-of-sight test on differently delivered attacks. Native Guard/Ward, Form/Catalyst activations and proc lineage remain with their existing owners; no new weapon hit or class cast is emitted. |
| Neural Arena observation | Game's local `outgoing_contact_requested(source_peer, target)` signal replaces Arena's removed `apply_weapon_hit` override. `ArenaWorld._observe_outgoing_contact` forwards it to `bridge.note_hit` without applying damage. There is no new packet. The actual Arena world/hook is exercised by `outgoing_hits`; this checkout lacks optional `mob_sim/bin/mob_nav.dll`, so its fixture uses an explicit observer witness and does not certify native simulation credit. If the backend is installed, the same fixture checks its real public damage events. |
| Remaining Game integration | The weapon slice below removes launch/projectile advancement. Game still coordinates pressure/threat/learning counters, death presentation, rewards and encounter completion. The bridge invokes `record_enemy_death`; MonsterRewards remains the one receipt owner. No HP, reward, charge, class profile or acquisition cache is duplicated. |

Measurements from `architecture/pre-outgoing-hits-20261118` (`9fd867b3dc69eacf2006140ac299dec0e7be3bf8`): Game **3,942 → 3,803 lines**, **250 → 248 functions**, **101 → 101 plain/onready variables**, with one local observation signal added. The two removed methods occupy 140 lines; the replacement is a 138-line policy and 228-line integration port. Total runtime text grows; the improvement is one independently readable execution sequence and scalar-source entry points instead of duplicate Game calculations. The port has 60 literal world-member references; these are relocated integration dependencies, not 60 eliminated dependencies. Its three lazy script-resource references cache immutable handler resources only, never player/world state, and avoid repeated loader lookups or preload cycles.

Seventeen runtime call sites in twelve consumer files now pass a peer identity instead of a mutable player record. The old Arena override is replaced by the local observer signal. Main, packet layouts, real profiles and protected tags are preserved.

Validation ownership follows the new files in the existing `combat` domain, with `arena_combat_bridge` identifying the touched Arena integration. The registry grows **226 → 227** with the new `outgoing_hits` fixture. Thirty-one Game overlays and both old function mappings are retired. Thirteen existing scoped anchors/normalization records change only their obsolete call syntax; unrelated historical baseline text is retained. The baseline elite fixture rendered a collision-only map with no ExitGate; it now restores a complete authored floor before its visual assertion, preserving all combat assertions.

The fixed contact workload is 1,600 nonlethal 10-HP leader hits, zero push, empty scout roster and no plant/card/graft effects; the first 200 warm up, then seven 200-hit batches are timed. The original median is **37.1 microseconds/contact**; the scoped implementation is approximately **53 microseconds/contact**, after removing repeated script lookups (first version: 56.6). This is about 0.016 ms additional contact work, not evidence of a frame-rate gain. Full-frame, terrain generation and snapshot encoding are outside this measurement. Broader performance optimization remains separate from correctness and readability.

Final focused evidence passes **36 suites / 45 strict process verdicts**, plus **106 validation-tool self-tests**, with zero ownership/scope warnings. Coverage includes all five connected classes, all nine affinity families, current cards/grafts, TC01, mitigation, support/elite/boss mechanics, source/spawn invalidation, exactly-once death receipts, sustain, actual Garden/Wilds entry, guarded rollback, five independent host/guest pairs and a four-player control with fifth-client refusal. Every data root is disposable under `.godot/vd`; this is automated local loopback, not manual or separate-PC/internet certification. The connected impact is **220 of 227** suites because Game's shared declarations/signatures changed. The remaining broad release-evidence refresh is not performed. Original failing fixture/diagnostic logs are retained.

Protocol 41, all 25 RPC signatures, compact snapshots, gameplay values and production save gates remain unchanged. Three card consumer-location annotations now point to `skill_targets`; their numbers/policies are unchanged, but the existing definitions fingerprint changes, requiring matching updated builds. Production AC-06 activation and host-crash recovery are not part of this extraction.

## Ordinary weapon launch and projectile lifecycle

[`weapon_attack_queue.gd`](../weapon_attack_queue.gd) is the sole owner of pending ordinary melee/projectile attacks. It receives [`WeaponAttackWorld`](../weapon_attack_world.gd), a focused integration port, rather than Game or mutable actors. Input requests a launch; the owner captures the build/roll, commits the existing action timeline, schedules contact, samples cursor aim at release, advances rays, and retires completed/cancelled records. Game wires mouse input and calls one ordered `advance` stage. Core/class queues retain their own delivery and are not ordinary weapon records.

| Interface / editing location | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `try_start`, `schedule`, `advance` | Admission, frozen launch stats, sequence-bound release, muzzle/terrain paths, fan expansion, shared target budget, contact order and completion. Times are combat milliseconds; distances are metres. `schedule` also supports explicit delivery fixtures without accepting mutable actors. |
| `pending_count`, `is_empty`, `views`, `inspect` | Cheap queue count or detached read-only inspection. Event handles are projected as scalar identity metadata. A view cannot be submitted back as a combat event or used to mutate a pending shot. The frame loop retains the old shallow iteration snapshot; it makes no deep queue/presentation copies. |
| `clear`, `savepoint`, `restore_savepoint` | Floor cleanup and transaction rollback. Savepoints are controlled transaction data, not presentation or network requests. Restore reconstructs shared fan-hit groups and never reuses an aborted queue ID. A revision change stops an obsolete in-progress iteration. Both guarded runtime capture and the still-supported compatibility transaction route restore this owner. |
| `weapon_attack_world.gd` | Scoped actor/owner observations, Form/graft/Reaper capture, native timeline operations, targeting/collision and presentation calls. It returns no mutable actor/profile, owner service, or private queue record. The queue never owns another player, roster, event budget or reward ledger. |
| Existing combat owners | `weapon_contact.gd` retains native melee/chain/splash geometry; `outgoing_hits.gd` resolves damage and attribution. `ranger_combat.gd::launch_grants` consumes Ranger preparations and returns contributions; the queue creates the extra projectile. Spirit claims count primary volley lanes and exclude the separately classified secondary projectile. |
| Authoritative tuning | Weapon stats remain in `class_catalog.gd`; delivery timing, speed, spread and radius remain in `weapon_motion.gd`. Card numbers stay in `affinity_card_catalog.gd`; the two weapon-card consumer annotations now point to `WeaponAttackWorld.capture_attack`. No balance value moved into the queue. |

The raw `Game.projectile_queue`, `Game.strike_enemy`, `Game.update_projectiles` and five MouseCombat delivery helpers are retired. MouseCombat retains held/cursor input, aim presentation and the shared pure TC01 path queries. Existing collision, contact budget, RNG-roll order, native attack identity and secondary attribution remain intact. There are no new packets or gameplay modes. Stale source life/floor/connection bindings are rejected; a fan retains its one shared contact set after rollback.

| Consistent measurement from `architecture/pre-weapon-lifecycle-20261119` (`f1ac31f14c8c7ccd206ab7e35eeb6015736a7c92`) | Before | After |
|---|---:|---:|
| Game total lines / functions / plain and onready variables | 3,803 / 248 / 101 | 3,712 / 246 / 102 |
| MouseCombat total lines / functions | 203 / 16 | 99 / 11 |
| Direct raw-queue mentions across Game, MouseCombat and diagnostics | 15 | 0 |
| MouseCombat literal Game-member mentions | 69 | 38 |

The extra Game variable replaces one mutable array with references to its owner and integration port, not a second queue. The owner is 229 lines with zero Game-member references; its port is 167 lines with 26 public operations. The port has 38 literal world-member mentions: 16 direct record/event-data accesses, nine owner accesses, one service reference, ten commands and two Godot transport checks. Actor timeline updates remain in that bridge. Combined MouseCombat/port mentions rise from 69 to 76; these are relocated integration dependencies, not 38 eliminated dependencies. Total affected runtime text grows by 201 lines. The substantive improvement is private state and one readable launch-to-completion sequence, not a claim of whole-project code reduction.

Focused evidence: **38 suites / 50 strict process verdicts**, including all fifteen weapon forms, five class trees, nine affinity families, current cards/grafts, TC01, duplicate/secondary events, source/spawn invalidation, both queue rollback routes, eight independent host/guest pairs and four-player combat with fifth-player refusal. The registry grows **227 → 228**. Thirty-four replaced source overlays and the old Game function mappings are retired; four retained operation anchors are narrowly rerouted and two pure-query anchors lose only retired queue headers. `tests/support/weapon_attack_case.gd` uses owner operations. `weapon_event_case.gd` observes events at admission for replay tests without exposing queue internals. Rendering fixtures restore complete authored landmarks before visual assertions. All **106 validation-tool tests** pass.

One baseline issue remains outside this boundary: `guarded_expedition` already failed its combined frozen-XP/trophy/adaptation/cue assertion before editing. It is not relabelled as a pass; the actual new guarded queue rollback, compatibility serializer rollback and independent guarded departure checks pass. Production AC-06 writers remain disabled, and host-crash loss of uncommitted work remains unresolved.

Launch attempts include cooldown refusals. These are workload-local CPU observations, not GPU/FPS, packet-encoding or separate-PC measurements. ## Shared resolved combat values

Starting point: `000a88669ef976821e1367fe418dc79752108ccf`, preserved by `architecture/pre-resolved-combat-build-20261119`. [`resolved_combat_build.gd`](../resolved_combat_build.gd) is the shared calculation path for actual basic attacks, card-adjusted native Q/R definitions and their displayed results. It receives class ID, weapon index, three part IDs, `Classes.combat_profile()` and immutable card/graft ledger references. It receives no Game, actor, current HP, RNG, timer, or cast/life identity.

| Operation / caller | Contract |
|---|---|
| `Cache.resolve`, `Cache.received` | Host resolves explicit build facts; guests reconstruct values from the existing accepted native-stats/card-details projection. Deep-copied keys detect in-place class/path/equipment/detail changes; new ledger references detect acquisition changes. Active and prepared equipment are distinct inputs. The eight-entry cache evicts only reproducible values. |
| `Snapshot.native_stats`, `weapon_base_stats`, `basic_stats` | Detached views respectively exclude basic-only bonuses, include basic graft bonuses before acquired cards for offer/headroom calculations, and include actual acquired weapon-card contributions for launch/Stats. This keeps ordinary reach/damage out of native class actions. |
| `class_skill`, `class_skills`, `tooltips`, `core_skill`, `refusal` | Detached resolved class definitions, lazily cached descriptions and the existing base Core kit/admission reason. Class-card tuning is the pure `AffinityCards.tune_class_contributions`. Affinity potency and action-source capture still occur in their existing handlers. Per-cast class preparations are not cached. |
| `dodge_ms`, `graft_value_resolver` | Share the existing bounded dodge composition and prospective graft quote. The latter receives base/lost/temporary health as scalars; it cannot read or change a live actor. Existing ledgers own accepted acquisitions and the transaction installs successors. |
| `Game.combat_build`, ClassCaster, WeaponAttackWorld, CoreCombat, loadout/Tab | The Game integration gathers facts once per query; specialized callers use the appropriate view. The native `Game.weapon_stats` wire shape remains intact. Detached UI facts run through the same pure calculation without giving views world access. |

The old Game-taking `Skills.wild_tooltips` and `class_skill_tooltip_stats` metadata cache, CoreCombat's repeated `equipped` resolver, and two duplicated AffinityBuild graft-quote helpers are retired. The former tooltip key omitted card/graft changes; the new view updates when those inputs change. Class R cards now appear in descriptions instead of being replaced by an untuned adapter. Oracle's already-working flat Ward-duration card is included in its tooltip through the same `class_skill_balance.gd::protection_ms` calculation used by the real grant. Actual damage, intervals, Ward duration, class cooldown floors, rarity amounts and graft values are unchanged.
